Ultimately, this work underscores the potential of unlocking SAM3's latent capability for cross-image In-Context prompting, establishing a robust, training-free paradigm that effectively bridges the gap between general-purpose foundation models and specialized, label-intensive perception tasks without the need for task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [182] [Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11754)
*Jordão Bragantini,Ilan Theodoro,Loïc A. Royer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HOCT模型，通过基于3D几何先验的边中心架构解决细胞追踪中的两个结构障碍（谱系路径纠缠和边标签随机性），在细胞追踪挑战赛和细菌分裂基准测试中达到SOTA性能，且微调效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细胞追踪方法忽略候选追踪图中的两个结构问题：(i) 细胞分裂导致不同谱系路径在节点嵌入空间纠缠，(ii) 共享同一节点的边标签近似随机，使得图拓扑信息对GNN聚合无效。

Method: 提出HOCT（高阶细胞追踪Transformer），采用边中心架构，让候选细胞链接在3D几何先验下互相注意力机制，从而解决节点纠缠和边标签随机性问题。

Result: 在Cell Tracking Challenge和细菌分裂基准上取得SOTA结果，无需深度预训练图像编码器；在人机协同场景下仅用400个标注即可将追踪误差降低59%，相比LoRA微调其他Transformer基线提升6.75%。

Conclusion: HOCT通过边中心架构有效克服了候选追踪图的结构缺陷，在细胞追踪任务上超越了现有方法，且更适合少标注场景下的微调部署。

Abstract: Reconstructing lineages from live-imaging microscopy requires linking cell detections across time, including through cell divisions. A common approach is to construct a candidate graph and associate cell segmentations (nodes) across frames. However, these and other existing methods overlook two structural obstacles in candidate tracking graphs: (i) cell divisions entangle distinct lineage paths in the node embedding space, and (ii) edges sharing a node have near-random label agreement, so the candidate-graph topology carries no useful information for graph neural networks to aggregate. We propose the \textbf{Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer} (HOCT), an edge-centric architecture in which candidate cell links attend to one another under a 3D geometric prior, resolving both issues. Evaluated on the Cell Tracking Challenge and a bacteria division benchmark, HOCT achieves state-of-the-art results without deep pre-trained image encoders. Moreover, the proposed approach is easier to fine-tune, quickly reducing tracking errors by 59% with 400 annotations in a human-in-the-loop setting, outperforming LoRA fine-tuning of competing transformer baselines (6.75% improvement).

</details>


### [183] [MicroCharNet: Less is More for License Plate Character Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11830)
*Huy Che,Dinh-Duy Phan,Duc-Lung Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroCharNet，一个超轻量级车牌字符检测模型，仅需0.08M参数和0.096 GFLOPs，在UFPR-ALPR数据集上达到与YOLO系列相当的检测精度，适合在边缘设备上实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度车牌字符检测模型依赖大规模架构，计算开销大，难以部署到资源受限的边缘设备上，因此需要设计超轻量级模型以平衡精度与效率。

Method: 采用C2f模块构建紧凑骨干网络，集成CoordAtt模块增强特征提取并保留空间信息；使用轻量级C3k2-based neck融合多级特征；采用单层无锚点检测头实现端到端预测。

Result: 在UFPR-ALPR数据集上，MicroCharNet仅0.08M参数和0.096 GFLOPs，实现了与多个YOLO基线模型竞争甚至更优的检测精度，并在硬件级评估中验证了其在边缘设备上的实时部署效率。

Conclusion: 精心设计的超轻量级架构能够有效平衡车牌字符检测中的精度与效率，为资源受限场景下的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: License plate character detection is a crucial component of intelligent transportation systems, where high accuracy and computational efficiency are required for real-time deployment. Although recent deep learning-based methods have substantially improved detection performance, many high-accuracy models rely on large-scale architectures that incur substantial computational overhead, limiting their applicability to resource-constrained devices. In this paper, we propose MicroCharNet, an ultra-lightweight model specifically designed for license plate character detection. The proposed architecture employs a compact backbone composed of C2f blocks, integrated with CoordAtt module to enhance feature extraction while preserving spatial information. A lightweight C3k2-based neck fuses multi-level features, followed by a single-level anchor-free detection head that enables end-to-end prediction. Experiments conducted on the UFPR-ALPR dataset demonstrate that MicroCharNet achieves competitive detection accuracy with only 0.08M parameters and 0.096 GFLOPs, while outperforming several recent YOLO-based baselines. Hardware-level evaluations further confirm its efficiency for real-time deployment on edge devices. These results indicate that carefully designed ultra-lightweight architectures can effectively balance accuracy and efficiency in license plate character detection. The source code is available at https://github.com/chequanghuy/MicroCharNet.

</details>


### [184] [Cycle-World: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Long-term Video World Models via Reverse-Prediction Cycle Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11836)
*Zihan Su,Teng Hu,Jiangning Zhang,Ruiyan Wang,Ran Yi,Lizhuang Ma,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cycle-World提出了一种通过循环一致性约束和反向预测模型来抑制自回归扩散模型在长视频生成中误差累积的框架，在60秒视频合成中达到了SOTA质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩散模型在长视频生成中面临严重的误差累积问题，微小的预测偏差随时间叠加会导致生成漂移、结构塌陷和视觉退化。

Method: 在训练阶段引入反向预测模型，通过循环一致性目标将因果约束隐式嵌入前向生成器；在推理阶段将冻结的反向模型作为运行时校正器，通过基于梯度的循环引导迭代优化潜在表示，主动抑制累积误差。

Result: 在VBench基准上，Cycle-World显著缓解了误差漂移，在60秒长视频合成中达到了整体生成质量和长时间一致性的最优结果。

Conclusion: 双阶段协同（训练时嵌入因果约束 + 推理时循环校正）能够有效抑制长视频生成中的误差累积问题，验证了时间可逆性约束对稳定长时生成的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation, yet their sequential nature inherently suffers from error accumulation. In long-horizon video synthesis, minor prediction deviations compound over time, inevitably leading to unconstrained generative drift, structural collapse, and severe visual degradation. To address this, we propose Cycle-World, a novel framework designed for stable and temporally consistent long-video generation. Our approach tackles error drift by enforcing strict temporal reversibility across both the training and inference phases. Theoretically, we demonstrate that forward generative drift can be strictly bottlenecked by a cycle-consistency objective. During training, we integrate an efficient reverse-prediction model to implicitly embed causal constraints into the forward generator, compelling it to produce reversible sequences that tightly adhere to the natural video manifold. At inference time, we repurpose this frozen reverse model as a runtime corrector. Through gradient-based cycle guidance, it iteratively refines the generated latent representations, actively suppressing accumulated errors before they are committed to the historical context. Extensive experiments on the VBench benchmark demonstrate that Cycle-World's dual-phase synergy significantly mitigates error drift, achieving state-of-the-art overall generation quality and long-horizon temporal consistency in 60-second synthesis.

</details>


### [185] [HASTE: A Platform for Rapid Post-Disaster Building Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11838)
*Caleb Robinson,Anthony Ortiz,Simone Fobi Nsutezo,Cameron Birge,Meygha Machado,Marcelo Duarte,Joaquin Rivero Rodriguez,Anthony Cintron Roman,Kevin White,Inbal Becker-Reshef,Juan M. Lavista Ferres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HASTE是一个无代码网页平台，让非机器学习专家的分析师能在灾后数小时内，仅用灾后卫星影像生成逐建筑损伤地图。它提供两种方法：一是用户标注多边形后训练小语义分割模型；二是用预训练视觉模型嵌入建筑足迹，少量标注后拟合逻辑回归。实验表明其基础模型嵌入方法以二十分之一的标签量达到全监督ResNet-50基线水平。自2023年起已支持30余次真实灾害响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型灾害发生后，响应者需要在数小时内获得建筑损毁地图。现有高性能模型依赖匹配的灾前灾后影像和来自相似历史事件的训练集，这些在新灾害发生第一天通常不可用。因此需要一个让非机器学习专家的分析师也能快速生成损毁地图的平台。

Method: HASTE平台包含两种方法：1) 用户标注灾后影像中的多边形区域，训练小规模语义分割模型，逐像素预测后与现有建筑足迹结合；2) 用预训练视觉模型对每个建筑足迹进行嵌入，用户标注少量建筑后，在浏览器中拟合逻辑回归，数秒内评分整个场景。两种方法共享统一界面。

Result: 在xBD数据集上的初步实验表明，基础模型嵌入方法仅需全监督ResNet-50基线二十分之一的标签量，即可区分受损与完好建筑。自2023年以来已支持30余次真实灾害响应（地震、飓风、龙卷风、洪水、野火、龙卷风），在影像可用后数小时至数天内交付结果。

Conclusion: HASTE是一个开源平台，证明了在无灾前影像和少量标注条件下进行快速灾害评估的可行性。未来方向包括视觉语言评估、主动学习以及针对道路等基础设施的损伤模型。

Abstract: When a large disaster strikes, responders need a map of which buildings are damaged within hours. The models that do well on public benchmarks assume matched before-and-after imagery and a training set drawn from similar past events, and neither is usually available for a new disaster in its first day. We present HASTE (High-speed Assessment and Satellite Tracking for Emergencies), a no-code web platform that lets analysts who are not machine learning engineers produce per-building damage maps from post-disaster satellite imagery. HASTE implements two methods that share one interface. The first requires the user to label polygons over the post-disaster scene, trains a small semantic segmentation model on that single scene, runs it over the whole image, and joins the per-pixel output to existing building footprints. The second embeds every footprint with a pretrained vision model, requires the user to label a handful of buildings, and fits a logistic regression in the browser that scores the rest of the scene in seconds. We describe the platform, both methods, and the engineering that supports them. We also report preliminary experiments on xBD showing that foundation-model embeddings pooled over footprints separate damaged from intact buildings using post-disaster imagery alone, matching a fully supervised ResNet-50 baseline with a twentieth of its labels. HASTE and its predecessors have supported more than thirty real-world disaster responses since 2023, spanning earthquakes, hurricanes, cyclones, floods, wildfires, and tornadoes, delivering results to humanitarian partners within hours to days of imagery becoming available. We close with the directions we think are most promising, including vision-language assessment, active learning, and damage models for roads and other infrastructure. HASTE is open source at https://github.com/microsoft/haste.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond the Single Camera: Agentic Multi-View Reasoning in Sports Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11844)
*Kerui Chen,Jinglu Wang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SportMV-Bench，首个评估多模态大模型在多视角体育视频理解能力的基准，包含787个多视角视频和2592个问答对。现有MLLM在多视角信息利用上表现不佳，瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知和视角选择。作者提出的SportMV-Agent框架通过主动视角选择、感知工具执行和基于证据的推理循环，相比最强基线提升了14.46%的相对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在单视角视频理解上表现优秀，但体育视频存在密集遮挡、快速运动和复杂交互，单视角难以解决。实践中体育比赛使用多视角录制，但尚无基准评估MLLM在多视角体育视频理解上的能力。

Method: 1) 构建SportMV-Bench基准：采用LLM生成、MLLM验证和人工过滤的pipeline，从官方比赛录像中构建787个多视角视频和2592个问答对，覆盖三类任务（PAR、REI、ADR）。2) 提出SportMV-Agent框架：迭代循环进行主动视角选择、感知工具执行和基于证据的推理。

Result: 当前MLLM在多视角体育视频理解上表现不佳，瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知和视角选择，而非逻辑推理或领域知识。SportMV-Agent相比最强MLLM基线实现了14.46%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 多视角信息对体育视频理解至关重要，现有MLLM在此任务上存在明显短板。通过主动视角选择和工具增强的agent框架可有效提升性能，未来需进一步解决细粒度视觉感知和视角选择问题。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on single-view video understanding benchmarks. However, sports videos involve dense occlusion, rapid motion, and complex interactions that are difficult to resolve from a single viewpoint. In practice, sports events are recorded from multiple camera angles, providing complementary evidence used by referees. Yet, no existing benchmark evaluates MLLMs on multi-view sports video understanding. To address this gap, we introduce SportMV-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark built from official match recordings, through a dedicated pipeline combining LLM-based generation, MLLM-based verification, and human filtering to ensure quality and consistency. SportMV-Bench containing 787 multi-view video bundles and 2592 question-answer pairs across three categories: Perception-Aware Recognition (PAR), Rule-aware Event Interpretation (REI), and Adjudicative Decision Reasoning(ADR). Our analysis shows that current MLLMs fail to effectively exploit multi-view information, with the bottlenecks lying in fine-grained visual perception and view selection rather than logical reasoning or domain knowledge. We propose SportMV-Agent, an agentic framework that orchestrates an iterative loop of active view selection, perception tool execution, and evidence-grounded reasoning, achieving a significant 14.46% relative improvement over the strongest MLLM baseline.

</details>


### [187] [Latent-Identity Tuning in Text-to-Image Personalization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11885)
*Daniel Garibi,Ronen Kamenetsky,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需额外训练的细粒度身份调优方法，通过在冻结编码器的潜在空间中探索语义方向，实现对特定身份的面部进行局部、精细且语义一致的编辑，同时保持跨图像的身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用文本到图像模型的个性化和编辑方法缺乏对面部进行精细编辑所需的精度，因为即使微小的修改也可能显著改变人物的身份感知。

Method: 利用预训练冻结编码器的潜在空间，通过识别潜在语义方向（特别是由不同潜变量 tokens 定义的子空间）来实现局部化的身份调优，无需额外训练。

Result: 定性和定量实验表明，该方法能够实现多样化的局部面部编辑，同时保持跨图像的身份一致性。

Conclusion: 在冻结编码器的潜在空间中存在有意义的语义方向，可用于精细粒度的身份编辑，为无需重新训练的面部个性化编辑提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Generating and editing a person's face demands high precision, as even minor modifications can significantly alter a subject's perceived identity. Current personalization and editing methods built on general-purpose text-to-image models, however, often lack the precision required for fine-grained facial edits. We present a method for fine-grained identity tuning in text-to-image personalization models. Unlike standard image editing, which operates on a given image, identity tuning modifies the latent representation of a specific identity, enabling the generation of diverse images that consistently depict the same edited identity. To enable fine-grained latent identity tuning, we explore the latent space of a pre-trained, frozen encoder for text-to-image personalization. Our approach requires no additional training. Instead, it leverages the existing architecture of a frozen encoder to uncover latent semantic directions. This space consists of a set of latent tokens that play distinct roles in capturing different aspects of an identity and often correspond to specific spatial or semantic facial regions. We show that meaningful directions can be identified within this space and within subspaces defined by selected tokens, enabling localized, fine-grained, and semantically coherent edits. We validate our approach through qualitative and quantitative experiments that demonstrate diverse localized facial edits while preserving cross-image identity consistency. Project page at: https://garibida.github.io/IdentityTuning/

</details>


### [188] [Read It Back: Pretrained MLLMs Are Zero-Shot Reward Models for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11886)
*Runhui Huang,Qihui Zhang,Zhe Liu,Yu Gao,Jie Wu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpectraReward，一种无需训练即可将预训练MLLM用作图像生成强化学习奖励函数的方法，通过计算生成图像对原始提示的重建对数似然作为奖励，并进一步提出Self-SpectraReward实现闭环自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM用作奖励模型需要偏好标注或微调，成本高且泛化能力有限。希望利用MLLM已有的图文对齐能力，无需额外训练即可获得有效的奖励信号。

Method: SpectraReward通过单次teacher-forced前向传播，计算从生成图像中恢复原始prompt的平均条件对数似然作为奖励。Self-SpectraReward则让统一多模态模型的理解分支为生成分支提供奖励，形成闭环。

Result: 在2种扩散模型、3种RL算法、9种MLLM骨干（4B-235B）和5个OOD基准上，显著且一致地提升了生成性能，超越了先前MLLM衍生奖励方法。

Conclusion: 更大的奖励MLLM不一定更好；Self-SpectraReward可匹配甚至超越更大的外部奖励模型，说明奖励-策略对齐是图像生成强化学习的关键因素。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose SpectraReward, a training-free reward function that turns pretrained MLLMs into off-the-shelf reward models for image-generation reinforcement learning. Instead of asking the MLLM to judge a generated image or answer decomposed verification questions, SpectraReward measures how well the original prompt can be recovered from the generated image through a single image-conditioned, teacher-forced forward pass. We use the average image-conditioned prompt log-likelihood as the reward, directly reusing the MLLM's pretrained image-text alignment ability without preference labels, reward-model fine-tuning. We further introduce Self-SpectraReward, a special case for unified multimodal models where the policy's own understanding branch serves as the reward model for its generation branch, forming a closed-loop self-improving framework without external reward models or external knowledge. Extensive experiments validate SpectraReward through a broad image-generation RL study covering two diffusion models, three RL algorithms, nine reward MLLM backbones from four MLLM families spanning 4B to 235B parameters, and five out-of-distribution text-to-image benchmarks. Results show that both SpectraReward and Self-SpectraReward significantly and consistently improve generation performance and outperform prior MLLM-derived reward training methods. Further analysis reveals that larger reward MLLMs are not always better, while Self-SpectraReward can match or surpass much larger external reward models, suggesting that reward-policy alignment is a key factor for effective image-generation RL. Project Page: https://huangrh99.github.io/SpectraReward/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [EgoSteer: A Full-Stack System Towards Steerable Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09701)
*Yifan Zhong,Zhang Chen,Tianrui Guan,Fanlian Zeng,Yuyao Ye,Tianjia He,Ka Nam Lui,Jiayi Li,Tingrui Zhang,Ruilin Yan,Xinhao Ji,Guangyu Zhao,Wenjie Lou,Jiayuan Zhang,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoSteer，一个全栈系统，通过从第一人称人类视频中扩展灵巧手VLA预训练，解决了灵巧手机器人缺乏大规模、语言对齐、动作精确的演示数据问题，实现了40+任务上的自由形式指令执行，以及在两个实体上75%+成功率的少样本长程任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手通用机器人策略缺乏可操控性（steerability），根本原因在于缺少大规模、语言对齐且动作精确的演示数据，现有方法无法有效利用海量人类视频进行预训练。

Method: 提出全栈系统：1) EgoSmith数据管道，从野外第一人称视频中筛选出9.6K小时高质量预训练数据，吞吐量提升9倍；2) 统一遥控操作+人在环校正机器人栈；3) EgoSteer，世界模型增强的VLA模型。先人类数据预训练获得语言引导的操作先验，再通过机器人后训练和DAgger细化进行落地。

Result: 在40+多样化任务上鲁棒执行自由形式指令，具备失败恢复、灵巧性和泛化能力；预训练模型在两个实体上实现75%+成功率的少样本长程任务（如折纸盒）适应。

Conclusion: 全栈方案有效打通了从人类视频到灵巧手VLA的规模化路径，开源了系统、数据和模型，为灵巧手通用策略提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Steerability is a defining capability of generalist robot policies, yet remains largely absent in dexterous-hand systems for lack of large-scale, language-aligned, and action-accurate demonstration data. To address this bottleneck, we present a full-stack system that scales dexterous VLA pre-training from egocentric human videos and enables data-efficient real-robot post-training. It integrates EgoSmith, a data pipeline that curates in-the-wild egocentric videos into 9.6K hours of high-quality pre-training data with 9x higher throughput and better accuracy than prior SOTA; a unified robot stack for teleoperation and human-in-the-loop correction; and EgoSteer, a world-model-enhanced VLA trained on optimized infrastructure. Human-data pre-training equips EgoSteer with language-guided manipulation priors, which are grounded through robot post-training and improved by DAgger refinement. Empirically, EgoSteer robustly executes free-form instructions across 40+ diverse tasks, demonstrating failure recovery, dexterity, and generalization. The pre-trained model also few-shot adapts to complex long-horizon tasks, including box folding, on two embodiments with 75+% success. We open-source the system, data, and model at https://egosteer.github.io/.

</details>


### [190] [RoboNav-Arm: Agentic AI-Driven Navigation and Obstacle Avoidance for Robotic Manipulator in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09716)
*Aachal Sharma,Narendra Kumar Dhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向非结构化环境的机器人操控框架，结合实时障碍物检测、语义报告生成、中央协调与自适应运动规划算法选择，实现了安全的目标导向任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖先验知识或固定感知管道，无法适应非结构化环境中的动态障碍物，导致安全执行目标任务困难。

Method: 框架包含环境模块（实时障碍检测、3D定位、地面几何估计与语义报告生成）、中央协调模块（工具调用、模块通信、任务监控）和规划模块（根据环境配置自适应选择RRTConnect等运动规划算法并进行轨迹优化）。

Result: 在Gazebo Classic仿真环境中进行了评估，验证了该方法在动态场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过模块化设计与自适应规划策略，有效提升了机械臂在非结构化环境中执行任务的避障能力与安全性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators operating in unstructured environments face significant challenges in safely executing goal-directed tasks due to dynamic and unforeseen obstacles, while traditional methods rely on prior knowledge or fixed perception pipelines, limiting adaptability. We propose a framework for safe task execution with effective obstacle avoidance. The environment module performs real-time obstacle detection, 3D localization, and ground surface geometry estimation. It then generates a structured semantic report that includes obstacle positions, object geometry and shape, and whether obstacles lie inside, outside, or within critical interaction zones. A central coordination module manages the overall system by handling tool invocation (e.g., memory and MoveIt collision scene updates), facilitating communication between modules, and continuously monitoring task progress until completion. Furthermore, a planning module selects an appropriate motion planning algorithm, such as RRTConnect, RRT*, or BiTRRT, based on the current environment configuration and goal requirements. The trajectory generated by the planner is further analyzed and refined to ensure safe and collision-free task execution. The proposed approach is evaluated in Gazebo Classic , demonstrating robustness in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [Learning High-Level Decision Making with an Interaction-Aware Attention-Based Network in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09725)
*Marcelo Contreras,Willi Poh,Christoph Stiller,Ehsan Hashemi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DecisionPerceiver架构，通过固定大小的潜在空间和更细粒度的动作离散化，在驾驶决策中实现了可扩展性和交互感知能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（DeepSet变体）忽略显式交通交互建模，限制了在谈判密集型场景（如交叉口）的表现；基于注意力的方法虽能捕获交互，但存在二次复杂度和表示粒度控制有限的问题。

Method: 受Perceiver IO启发，提出DecisionPerceiver架构，将动态智能体特征投影到固定大小的潜在空间，通过潜在查询数量调节特征粒度；同时提出更细粒度的动作离散化方案。

Result: 在三种不同交互意识需求的驾驶场景中，该方法展现出一致性的性能提升和跨导航目标的泛化能力；在车辆数量递增的场景中验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: DecisionPerceiver通过固定大小潜在空间解决了动态输入问题，兼顾了交互建模与计算效率，为自动化驾驶决策提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable learning-based high-level decision making for lane changes and speed control in automated driving must accommodate dynamically sized inputs due to varying scene traffic flow. DeepSet and its variants represent the state of the art among shared-encoder approaches; however, they neglect explicit traffic interaction modeling, limiting performance in negotiation-intensive scenarios such as intersections. Attention-based methods capture interactions among static and dynamic agents, but incur quadratic memory and computational complexity and provide limited control over representation granularity. Inspired by Perceiver IO, an attention-based architecture, DecisionPerceiver, is proposed to project dynamic agent features into a fixed-size latent space, where feature granularity is regulated by the number of latent queries, improving scalability for larger networks. A finer discretization of the action set is further proposed to increase the performance gain due to interaction awareness. Extensive evaluations across three driving scenarios that require different levels of interaction awareness demonstrate consistent performance gains and generalization across various navigation objectives. In addition, the proposed architecture is assessed in scenarios with an increasing number of vehicles to demonstrate scalability.

</details>


### [192] [Saturation-Aware Robust Trajectory Optimization for Reusable Launch Vehicles via Differentiable Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09736)
*Liwei Chen,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微物理框架（DPTC），通过集成粒子集合与硬执行器饱和约束，实现对高攻角翻转机动中鲁棒轨迹的端到端优化，相比AD-SCvx基线方法在气动扰动下具有更强的抗饱和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重复使用运载火箭在高攻角翻转机动中面临强非线性动力学、气动不确定性和执行器饱和等多重挑战，现有鲁棒轨迹优化方法难以同时处理这些约束。

Method: 提出可微粒子管控制（DPTC）框架：用拉格朗日粒子集合表示状态不确定性，将硬执行器投影算子嵌入计算图，通过端到端反向传播联合优化标称前馈轨迹和时变反馈策略。

Result: 六自由度蒙特卡洛仿真表明，基线AD-SCvx方法在气动扰动下因无约束反馈易导致执行器饱和，而DPTC主动进行约束感知的性能权衡，通过放松空间跟踪保留关键控制权限，鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: 将可微物理与基于集合的优化相结合，为高约束航空航天飞行系统提供了有效的鲁棒制导框架。

Abstract: The high-angle-of-attack flip maneuver of reusable launch vehicles presents significant challenges for robust trajectory optimization due to the combined effects of highly nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic uncertainties, and actuator saturation. This paper presents a differentiable physics framework for saturation-aware robust trajectory optimization. At its core, a Differentiable Particle Tube Control (DPTC) scheme is developed to optimize uncertainty evolution through an ensemble-based distribution shaping strategy. State uncertainty is represented by a Lagrangian particle ensemble, while hard actuator projection operators are embedded directly into the computational graph, enabling the joint optimization of the nominal feedforward trajectory and a time-varying feedback policy via end-to-end backpropagation. The proposed framework is evaluated against an automatic differentiation-based Successive Convexification (AD-SCvx) baseline combined with a conventional covariance steering feedback strategy. Six-degree-of-freedom Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that, although the baseline achieves nominal fuel-optimal solutions, its unconstrained feedback formulation becomes susceptible to actuator saturation under aerodynamic disturbances, leading to degraded closed-loop robustness. In contrast, the proposed DPTC framework proactively performs a constraint-aware performance trade-off by relaxing spatial tracking to preserve critical control authority. These results demonstrate that integrating differentiable physics with ensemble-based optimization provides an effective and practical framework for robust guidance in highly constrained aerospace flight systems.

</details>


### [193] [SWIFT: A Small-World Interaction Framework for Flow-Aware Trajectory Prediction in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09741)
*Chengyue Wang,Bin Rao,Haicheng Liao,Bonan Wang,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIFT框架，将小世界网络与交通流理论结合用于自动驾驶轨迹预测，通过结构先验提升泛化能力，在多个数据集上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法纯数据驱动，缺乏结构先验，导致在分布偏移下泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出SWIFT框架，包含小世界交互网络（捕获局部与全局依赖）、流量机制编码器（适应场景级交通状态）和多关系图模块（显式编码直接与高阶代理关系）。

Result: 在nuScenes、MoCAD、NGSIM三个数据集上，SWIFT在多个交通场景下预测精度一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知的设计不仅提升了预测精度，还增强了跨场景泛化、噪声鲁棒性及小样本学习能力，验证了引入结构先验的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction in autonomous driving hinges on modeling dynamic and context-dependent interactions among traffic agents. However, most existing approaches are purely data-driven and lack structural priors, which limits their generalization under distribution shifts. In this work, interaction modeling is revisited through the structure and dynamics of traffic networks, and SWIFT (Small-World Interaction Framework for Trajectory prediction) is proposed as a unified framework that integrates small-world networks with traffic flow theory. SWIFT introduces structural inductive biases via a Small-World Interaction Network that captures both local and global dependencies, and a Flow Regime Encoder that adapts the interaction structure to scene-level traffic states. Interaction reasoning is further enhanced through a multi-relational graph module that explicitly encodes direct and higher-order agent relationships. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets, nuScenes, MoCAD, and NGSIM, show that SWIFT consistently outperforms strong baselines in prediction accuracy across diverse traffic regimes. Beyond accuracy, SWIFT exhibits improved generalization to unseen locations and regimes, robustness under noisy observations, and strong performance with limited training data, supporting the effectiveness of its structure-aware design.

</details>


### [194] [LLM-Centric Agentic AI for UAV Swarms: Architecture, Enabling Technologies, and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09756)
*Yousef Emami,Rahim Taheri,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Muhammad Atif Ur Rehman,Mohammad Shojafar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LAUS框架，将Agentic AI与大语言模型结合用于无人机集群，探讨了边缘计算、5G/6G连接、多模态智能和网络安全等关键技术，分析了优先级操控攻击等威胁，并指出了抗幻觉推理、SWaP约束下的机载LLM部署等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群在搜救和环境监测等领域潜力巨大，但受限于态势感知不足、间歇性连接和重大网络安全风险，实际部署面临瓶颈。

Method: 提出LLM-Centric Agentic AI for UAV Swarms (LAUS)框架，将大语言模型置于闭环认知架构核心，整合感知、记忆、推理/规划和行动，并综述了机载与边缘计算、5G/6G、多模态智能、网络安全等使能技术。

Result: 识别出优先级操控攻击(PMA)可扭曲决策并降级网络性能；揭示了Agentic AI在扩展无人机能力的同时也扩大了攻击面。

Conclusion: 指出开放研究方向：抗幻觉推理、SWaP约束下的机载LLM部署、感知-推理攻击的标准化安全基准。

Abstract: Uncrewed Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms have significant potential for applications such as Search and Rescue (SAR) and environmental monitoring, but their real-world deployment is limited by a lack of situational awareness, intermittent connectivity, and significant cybersecurity risks. Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) represents a shift from standalone Large Language Model (LLM) toward closed-loop cognitive architectures that integrate perception, memory, reasoning/planning, and action to enable adaptive, goal-directed swarm behavior. Within this framework, Agentic AI provides a unifying structure for autonomous and adaptive swarm operations while expanding the system attack surface compared to conventional AI systems. This paper proposes LLM-Centric Agentic AI for UAV Swarms (LAUS) and reviews key enabling technologies such as onboard and edge computing, 5G/6G connectivity, multimodal intelligence, and cybersecurity mechanisms, and analyzes threats such as Priority Manipulation Attacks (PMA) that can distort decision-making and degrade network performance. Finally, it identifies open research challenges, including hallucination-resistant reasoning, onboard LLM deployment under SWaP constraints, and standardized security benchmarks for perception-reasoning attacks in agentic UAV systems.

</details>


### [195] [OmniSCS: Omni Safety-Critical Scenario Synthesis for Autonomous Driving via a Fully Editable Driving World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09764)
*Xiaoyun Dong,Qian Xu,Yang Lu,Yang Lou,Yung-Hui Li,Jianping Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniSCS是一个能生成高保真安全关键场景并支持实时闭环测试的系统，通过双策略代理重建和深度精化背景重建，在场景编辑中保持数据保真度，在nuScenes、Waymo、KITTI数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在场景编辑后难以保持数据保真度，且无法高效生成高质量的物理逼真的安全关键场景用于闭环测试。

Method: 提出OmniSCS系统，包含两个核心模块：1）完全可编辑驾驶世界构建模块，通过双策略代理重建和深度精化背景重建保持高保真度；2）安全关键场景合成模块，支持物体插入和轨迹编辑以合成多样化安全关键场景。

Result: 在nuScenes、Waymo、KITTI三个数据集上，OmniSCS在编辑场景保真度方面优于现有方法，能增强自动驾驶算法，支持实时（13Hz）闭环测试。

Conclusion: OmniSCS为自动驾驶中的安全关键场景优化和测试提供了更安全、更有效且更具成本效益的解决方案。

Abstract: The synthesis of safety-critical scenarios (SCS) and their evaluation through closed-loop simulations are crucial for developing robust autonomous driving systems. A key aspect of this process involves editing agent states in both appearance and trajectory levels within existing scenes. However, current methods struggle to preserve data fidelity after scene editing and fail to efficiently generate high-quality SCS through such modifications. To overcome these limitations, we propose OmniSCS, an innovative system that generates photorealistic SCS with high physical fidelity while enabling closed-loop testing in synthetic environments. OmniSCS comprises two key modules: 1) A Fully Editable Driving World Construction module that maintains high-fidelity agent appearance and background during scene editing via dual-strategy agent reconstruction and depth-refinement background reconstruction methods. 2) A SCS Synthesis module that facilitates object insertion and agent trajectory editing to synthesize diverse SCS while preserving data fidelity. Experiments on nuScenes, Waymo, and KITTI datasets show that OmniSCS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in edited scene fidelity. We further validate its ability to enhance autonomous driving algorithms and support real-time (13Hz) closed-loop testing. Overall, OmniSCS provides a safer, more effective, and cost-efficient solution for SCS optimization and testing in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [196] [A Risk-Field Enhanced Closed-Loop Digital Twin Framework for Autonomous Driving Safety Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09772)
*Yongzhi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险场增强的闭环数字孪生框架，用于自动驾驶安全验证。该框架整合物理数据采集、同步、虚拟重建、风险场景生成、算法评估与安全分析，引入驾驶风险场作为统一中间表征，通过风险场排序高危险场景并为强化学习策略提供安全引导。实验表明，将显式风险结构嵌入数字孪生能使自动驾驶验证更具针对性、可解释性和可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模道路测试成本高、难以复现、效率低下，难以暴露罕见安全关键场景；传统离线仿真虽改善了可重复性，但无法持续连接物理交通状态、虚拟重建、算法评估和场景演化过程。

Method: 提出风险场增强的闭环数字孪生框架，包含数据采集与同步、虚拟孪生重建、风险感知场景生成、自动驾驶算法评估及安全分析；引入驾驶风险场作为统一中间表征，涵盖障碍物、车道偏离、道路边界、碰撞时间和舒适性风险；利用风险场对数字孪生场景库中的高风险场景排序，并为强化学习提供密集安全引导。

Result: 设计了仿真风格评估协议，比较了传统强化学习基线、风险惩罚基线与所提风险场引导方法。结果表明嵌入显式风险结构使自动驾驶验证更精准、可解释且可复用。

Conclusion: 风险场增强的数字孪生框架能有效提升自动驾驶安全验证的针对性与可解释性，但其实际效果受限于模型保真度、风险校准和仿真到现实迁移等因素。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems require reliable safety validation before real-world deployment. However, large-scale road testing is costly, difffcult to reproduce, and inefffcient for exposing rare safety-critical scenarios. Conventional simulation improves repeatability, but an offfine simulator alone cannot continuously connect physical trafffc states, virtual reconstruction, algorithm evaluation, and scenario evolution. This paper proposes a risk-ffeld enhanced closed-loop digital twin framework for autonomous driving safety validation. The framework integrates physical data acquisition, data synchronization, virtual twin reconstruction, risk-aware scenario generation, autonomous driving algorithm evaluation, and safety analysis. A driving risk ffeld is introduced as a uniffed intermediate representation to describe obstacle, lane-departure, road-boundary, time-to-collision, and comfort-related risks around the ego vehicle. The risk ffeld ranks high-risk scenarios in the digital twin scenario library and provides dense safety guidance for reinforcement learning-based driving policies. A simulation-style evaluation protocol is designed to compare conventional reinforcement learning baselines, risk-penalty baselines, and the proposed risk-ffeld guided method. The study indicates that embedding explicit risk structure into digital twins can make autonomous driving validation more targeted, interpretable, and reusable, while its practical effectiveness remains bounded by model ffdelity, risk calibration, and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [197] [Maximizing Human Efficiency in Large-Scale Robot Post-Training via VLAC-Cut Guided Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09776)
*Shaopeng Zhai,Qi Zhang,Tianyi Zhang,Haoran Zhang,Fuxian Huang,Zhanhui Lin,Zijun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效利用人力的VLA模型后训练流水线，通过角色分工（远程操作员+地面监控员）和自动数据筛选工具VLAC-CUT，实现少量人力监督多台机器人，在四个真实操作任务上最终策略成功率80%-95%，吞吐量提升1.7-4.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在下游任务中需要多轮后训练迭代，但单轮数据无法解决所有问题。现有方法人力成本高、效率低，亟需一种能在有限人力和时间投入下最大化策略改进和任务吞吐量的训练方案。

Method: 1) 分工明确的双角色协作：远程操作员负责高价值干预和恢复演示，地面监控员负责多机器人监控和物理复位；2) 自动数据筛选工具VLAC-CUT，将机器人自主轨迹分割为进展/空闲/失败/恢复四类，保留有用片段；3) 筛选后的数据与人在回路数据混合用于下一轮后训练。

Result: 在四个真实操作任务上，多轮迭代后策略成功率达80%-95%，任务吞吐量较基线提升1.7-4.2倍。在相同人力干预预算下，VLAC-CUT引导的轨迹复用优于纯人在回路训练。

Conclusion: 通过角色分工和自动数据筛选，该流水线显著降低了VLA模型后训练的人力成本，提升了数据利用效率和任务性能，为大规模机器人部署提供了一条高效可行的路径。

Abstract: When adapting Vision Language Action (VLA) models to downstream tasks, multiple rounds of post training are required because a single round of data cannot resolve all issues, making continuous iterations necessary to progressively address the weaknesses exposed in previous rounds. In this report, we aim to maximize human efficiency during post-training, defined as the policy improvement and task throughput achieved per unit of human labor and time.
  We propose a human-efficient post-training pipeline that enables a small number of human operators to supervise multiple robots. The pipeline is built around a specialized division of labor: a trained Teleoperator focuses on high-value remote interventions and recovery demonstrations, while a Floor Operator monitors multiple robots, triggers takeovers, and performs physical resets. This role specialization reduces task switching, lowers operator training costs, and allows limited human labor to supervise more robot interaction across a larger fleet. To improve data utilization efficiency, we introduce VLAC-CUT as an automatic rollout curation tool. It segments autonomous robot trajectories into progress-making, idle, failure-inducing, and recovery portions, preserving useful segments while filtering harmful or uninformative ones. The curated rollout data are combined with Human-in-the-Loop data for the next post-training round. We validate the proposed pipeline on four real-world manipulation tasks. Across iterative post-training rounds, the final policies achieve 80\%--95\% success rates and improve task throughput by 1.7$\times$--4.2$\times$ over the base model. Under the same human-intervention budget, VLAC-CUT guided rollout reuse outperforms HITL-only training in both success rate and throughput.

</details>


### [198] [A Comprehensive Survey and Systematic Real-World Evaluation of Embodied Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09792)
*Liuyi Wang,Kai Sheng,Zongtao He,Jinlong Li,Yongrui Qin,Haojie Dai,Xiangyi Wang,Jingwei Yang,Qingqing Yan,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了视觉与语言导航（VLN）的研究，按动作范式（层次化vs整体化）和模型范式（判别式vs生成式）两个正交维度对现有方法进行分类，并在真实机器人平台上进行了系统评估。实验表明，模拟环境与真实部署间存在显著性能差距（如RGB-only方法从61%成功率降至22%），而层次化框架在真实场景中表现更优（51%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统依赖高度结构化的模型和强先验假设，在开放不确定的真实环境中鲁棒性不足。尽管VLN结合了自然语言理解与视觉感知，但缺乏系统的方法论分类和真实世界验证。

Method: 从两个正交维度组织现有方法：动作范式（层次化框架vs整体化框架）和模型范式（判别式方法vs生成式方法）；并在物理机器人平台上对代表性系统配置进行系统化的真实世界评估。

Result: 在10个真实场景中：整体化RGB-only方法模拟成功率61%，真实部署仅22%；层次化框架真实部署成功率达51%，鲁棒性更强。模拟与真实部署之间存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: 指出了感知、决策和控制方面的关键挑战，认为层次化框架在真实环境中更具鲁棒性，未来需弥合仿真与真实部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Navigation is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems, yet most existing approaches rely on highly structured models and strong prior assumptions, limiting their robustness in open and uncertain real-world environments. Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) offers a promising direction by enabling robots to integrate natural language understanding with visual perception in a data-driven manner. Although VLN has attracted increasing research attention, systematic methodological taxonomy and real-world validation remain limited. This survey presents a comprehensive review of VLN research. Specifically, state-of-the-art methods are organized along two orthogonal dimensions: action paradigms, including hierarchical and monolithic frameworks, and model paradigms, including discriminative and generative approaches. A critical analysis of their respective strengths and limitations is provided. Additionally, we conduct a systematic real-world evaluation of representative VLN system configurations on a physical robotic platform. Experiments across ten diverse real-world scenes show a substantial performance gap between simulation and real-world deployment under the tested configurations: a representative monolithic RGB-only method achieves 61% success in simulation but drops to 22% in real-world deployment, while a hierarchical framework achieves a higher real-world success rate of 51%, suggesting stronger robustness in our evaluation setting. Finally, we highlight key challenges in perception, decision-making, and control that must be addressed in future research.

</details>


### [199] [A Biomimetic Myoelectric Tentacle Prosthesis with Sensorless Object Detection and Vibrotactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09807)
*Gabrielle Marion,Olivier Lecompte,Amandine Gesta,Abolfazl Mohebbi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种结合肌电控制、无传感器物体检测和振动触觉反馈的仿生触手假肢。该系统利用对数螺旋曲线实现柔性卷曲运动，通过电机电流斜率分析检测物体接触（无需外部传感器），并采用累积振动刺激传递空间信息。实验显示平均响应时间77ms，物体检测成功率超90%，用户可可靠识别触手折叠区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假肢多追求仿人形态，忽视了表达的灵活性与功能性。作者希望通过非拟人化的仿生设计，打造一种能够自适应不同物体形状、同时提供触觉反馈的响应式辅助设备，提升用户的交互体验和感知能力。

Method: 1) EMG信号归一化、滤波及阈值法识别用户意图；2) 对数螺旋曲线控制柔性触手卷曲运动；3) 基于电机电流斜率分析实现无传感器物体接触检测；4) 累积振动触觉刺激策略传递触手空间配置信息。

Result: 平均响应时间77ms，实时交互流畅；物体检测成功率超90%，对EMG变异性具有鲁棒性；用户可通过振动反馈可靠识别触手折叠区域。

Conclusion: 该仿生触手设计验证了非拟人化假肢的可行性，为未来更具表现力的假肢研究提供了新方向，即在功能性与表达性之间取得平衡，而非固守传统的人形外观。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and evaluation of a myoelectric tentacle-shaped prosthesis integrating electromyographic (EMG) control, sensorless object detection, and vibrotactile feedback. The objective was to develop a responsive and intuitive assistive device that adapts to various object shapes while providing sensory feedback to the user. The system relies on EMG signals to control the motion of a flexible, biomimetic structure whose curling geometry follows a logarithmic spiral, enabling it to coil around objects. To ensure stable control, the EMG signal is normalized and filtered, and a threshold-based method identifies user intention. Object contact is detected through a slope-based analysis of motor current, eliminating the need for external sensors, and a haptic feedback strategy based on cumulative vibrotactile stimulation conveys spatial information about the tentacle's configuration. The system was evaluated through quantitative and qualitative tests. The results demonstrate a low response time (77 ms on average), enabling smooth real-time interaction; an object-detection success rate above 90%, confirming robustness despite EMG variability; and an effective haptic feedback strategy that allowed users to reliably identify the folding zone of the tentacle. The proposed biomimetic design promotes further investigation of expressive artificial limbs by prioritizing expressive functionality over adherence to a predefined, anthropomorphic form factor.

</details>


### [200] [RASR: Range-Aware Scale Recovery for Metric UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09815)
*Hongtao Liang,Xinyu Shao,Chenxu Wang,Yiyao Wan,Jiahuan Ji,Fangwei Ye,Fuhui Zhou,Qihui Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Range-Aware Scale Recovery (RASR)，通过分离可迁移的尺度恢复核心与协议特定的校准模块，解决无人机在GNSS拒止环境下最后一米导航中的尺度偏差问题，在PairUAV评估中取得总分0.003189。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止环境下，无人机需要精确的度量级最后一米导航能力。现有的稠密双视角几何基础模型虽然能很好地传递相对结构信息，但其原始度量输出的距离尺度校准不佳，导致靠近目标时存在代价高昂的距离相关残差。

Method: RASR 将系统分为两部分：(1) 可迁移的尺度恢复核心，压缩冻结的 MASt3R 风格几何特征为紧凑描述符，利用全局校准恢复主导的度量信号；(2) 协议特定的校准模块，包含范围桶残差校正（range-bucket residual correction）和指令网格对齐（command-grid alignment），匹配PairUAV的指令格式和评估协议。推理时整个每对系统固定不变。

Result: 在 Multimedia 2026 PairUAV 在线评估中，RASR 达到总分0.003189。

Conclusion: 在PairUAV协议下，冻结的双视角几何能稳定产生每对的距离和航向估计，所有协议特定的调整都被限制在推理前固定的校准模块内，实现了尺度校正与协议适配的解耦。

Abstract: Under Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) denial, a UAV controller still needs a distance and heading command it can execute, making accurate metric last-meter navigation essential. Dense pair-geometry foundation models transfer relative structure well, yet the distance scale of their raw metric outputs remains poorly calibrated. Under the relative error metric of PairUAV, correcting only the average scale can still leave costly, distance-dependent residuals near the goal. To address this scale mismatch, Range-Aware Scale Recovery (RASR) separates a transferable scale-recovery core from a protocol-specific calibration module in a per-pair system fixed at inference. The core compresses frozen Matching And Stereo 3D Reconstruction (MASt3R)-style geometry into a compact descriptor and uses global calibration to recover the dominant metric signal. Range-bucket residual correction and command-grid alignment stay inside the calibration module, so they match the command format and evaluation protocol of PairUAV. On the UAVs in Multimedia 2026 PairUAV online evaluation, RASR reaches a total score of 0.003189. Under the PairUAV protocol, frozen pair geometry thus yields stable per-pair distance and heading estimates, while every protocol-specific adjustment stays confined to a calibration module fixed before inference. Code and materials are available at https://github.com/lht-research/rasr-pairuav.

</details>


### [201] [TS-Mask VLA: 2D Temporal-Spatial Masking for Vision-Language-Action Model with Effective Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09818)
*Shengzhuo Yang,Ronghao Yu,Chuanjie Lv,Linpeng Peng,Hang Yu,Jie Ren,Jiajun Lv,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-Mask VLA框架，通过离散扩散动作专家（含Bridge Attention条件桥接）和时空2D遮蔽策略，在LIBERO和CALVIN等基准上以0.5B参数超越更大规模模型，实现95.7%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归token化动作生成范式将动作生成简化为下一个token预测，缺乏对动作序列时空结构的显式建模以及视觉-语言表示与动作之间的解耦，限制了长程和复杂场景下的性能。

Method: (1) 离散扩散动作专家（Discrete Diffusion Action Expert）配合Bridge Attention条件桥接，实现VLM的多层条件化，生成更准确稳定的动作；(2) 时空2D遮蔽策略（temporal-spatial 2D masking），增强模型对跨时间依赖和跨维度耦合的理解，生成结构一致的动作序列。

Result: LIBERO上以0.5B参数达95.7%平均成功率，超越更大模型；CALVIN上达最佳平均序列长度4.19，展现强长程任务能力。

Conclusion: TS-Mask VLA通过扩散模型和时空遮蔽策略有效提升了VLA框架在长程复杂操作任务中的性能，验证了设计有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models aim to understand natural-language instructions and visual observations, and to generate and execute corresponding actions as embodied agents. Recently, autoregressive token-based action generation has driven the development of many representative VLA models. However, this paradigm often reduces action generation to next-token prediction, thereby lacking explicit modeling of the spatiotemporal structure of action sequences and the disentanglement between vision-language representations and actions, which can limit performance in long-horizon and complex scenarios. In this paper, we propose TS-Mask VLA, a vision-language-action framework for robot manipulation. TS-Mask VLA is built upon two key designs: (1) a Discrete Diffusion Action Expert equipped with a Bridge Attention conditioning bridge, which enables multi-layer conditioning from the VLM and facilitates more accurate and stable action generation; and (2) a temporal-spatial 2D masking strategy for discrete action tokens that strengthens the model's understanding of cross-time dependencies and inter-dimensional coupling, leading to more structurally consistent action sequences. We conduct extensive experiments on simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks. On LIBERO, TS-Mask VLA achieves a 95.7 percent average success rate with only 0.5B parameters, outperforming significantly larger models. On CALVIN, it attains the best average sequence length of 4.19 and strong long-horizon performance. Comprehensive analyses and ablations further validate the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [202] [More Structure, Not More Capacity: Object-Centric Representations for Visuomotor Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09825)
*Yi Li,Alexandre Chapin,Liming Chen,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现，在机器人操控任务中，使用冻结的物体中心化slot表示（SPOT: DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention）相比密集的DINO全局特征基线，成功率提升22.4%（55.0% vs 32.6%），且仅靠增加token数量无效。加入2D空间目标和原生分辨率渲染后，系统达到68.7%，接近3D oracle上限（71.7%）。通过自动运动学失败分类发现，空间对齐主要减少定位误差（Near-Miss），但对抓取失败（No-Grasp）无改善，而遮挡是更困难任务的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操控策略依赖预训练视觉模型，这些模型提供全局场景嵌入或密集patch网格，但两者都会混合任务相关与不相关的特征。作者希望探究物体中心化的slot表示是否能通过结构化分组特征来提升操控性能。

Method: 在ManiSkill3 PickCube-v1任务上，保持策略、目标token、渲染和标定不变，仅将编码器替换为冻结的物体中心化SPOT表示（DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention），并与密集DINO全局特征基线对比。进一步加入显式2D空间目标和原生分辨率渲染，并设计自动运动学失败分类法（区分Near-Miss和No-Grasp）进行分析。

Result: SPOT表示达到55.0±2.9%成功率，比密集DINO全局特征基线（32.6±1.5%）高22.4%。密集patch网格即使有16倍token也无提升。全系统（加入2D空间目标+原生分辨率）达到68.7±4.2%，接近3D oracle上限（71.7±4.1%）。失败分类显示空间对齐减少Near-Miss但对No-Grasp无改善，遮挡是StackCube-v1的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 物体中心化的slot表示在机器人操控中显著优于全局特征和密集patch网格，尤其是在保持编码器冻结的情况下。空间对齐能有效减少定位误差但无法解决抓取失败问题，遮挡是更复杂任务的核心挑战。该工作强调了结构化视觉表示在操控任务中的价值。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies rely on pre-trained vision models that give either a global scene embedding or a dense patch grid. Both mix task-relevant and task-irrelevant features. Object-centric slot representations are a structured alternative: they group features into a few per-object slots. We test what this structure buys on ManiSkill3 PickCube-v1, with a frozen encoder and a held-out-seed evaluation. Holding the policy, goal token, rendering, and calibration fixed and changing only the encoder, a frozen object-centric SPOT representation (DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention) reaches 55.0$\pm$2.9% success, 22.4% above a dense DINO global-feature baseline (32.6 $\pm$ 1.5%), with the same trainable policy and no encoder fine-tuning. More tokens alone do not help: a dense patch grid with 16x the tokens performs no better than the global feature. Adding an explicit 2D spatial goal and native-resolution rendering raises the full system to 68.7$\pm$4.2%, just below a privileged 3D-oracle upper bound (71.7$\pm$4.1%). An automated kinematic failure taxonomy then separates spatial-precision (Near-Miss) failures from object-tracking (No-Grasp) failures: spatial grounding reduces Near-Miss while leaving No- Grasp unchanged. The same taxonomy transfers to the harder StackCube-v1 and points to occlusion as the main bottleneck.

</details>


### [203] [Robo-ValueRL: Reliable Value Estimation for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09866)
*Wenke Xia,Pei Ren,Wenbo Yu,Yizhuo Zhang,Jifan Li,Yixue Zhang,Yinuo Zhao,Qingyang Gao,Jianlong Fu,Jian Tang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhengping Che,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robo-ValueRL框架，通过历史条件价值估计器与全局-局部可靠性指标，系统分析价值函数可靠性如何影响离线到在线强化学习策略的性能。实验表明可靠价值估计能显著提升策略预训练与在线改进效果，在精密芯片插入（86%成功率）和可泛化拆解任务（84%）上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习虽在机器人操作中前景广阔，但全栈复杂性导致复现和诊断困难。价值估计在策略改进中起核心作用，但价值函数可靠性如何影响策略优化这一关键问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Robo-ValueRL框架，包含：(1) 历史条件价值估计器；(2) 全局进度与局部偏好两个可靠性评估指标；(3) 质量条件一致性策略预训练；(4) 基于残差适配的在线微调模块。将价值可靠性分析融入策略预训练与在线改进的全流程。

Result: 在240小时离线数据和3000+在线轨迹上实验，发现策略性能与价值可靠性高度相关。可靠价值函数能更好估计动作质量，使离线RL优于质量无关的行为克隆，并通过优先利用高质量在线数据稳定改进。最终在精密芯片插入任务达86%成功率，可泛化拆解任务达84%。

Conclusion: 价值函数的可靠性是离线到在线强化学习策略优化的关键因素。可靠的价值引导能够更有效地利用异构机器人经验，实现从离线预训练到在线改进的一致性能提升。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning is promising for generalizable robotic manipulation, yet its full-stack complexity obscures reproduction and diagnosis. Within such systems, value estimation plays a central role in prioritizing heterogeneous data for policy improvement. Despite its importance, the central question remains underexplored: how value-function reliability shapes policy optimization in offline-to-online reinforcement learning. To answer this question, we propose Robo-ValueRL, a unified framework that enables reliable value estimation and systematically traces its downstream effects on policy pretraining and online improvement. Concretely, Robo-ValueRL learns a history-conditioned value estimator and evaluates its reliability through global-progress and local-preference metrics. These resulting value estimates are propagated into quality-conditioned consistency-policy pretraining and a residual adaptation module on online rollouts, providing a unified testbed for analyzing how value reliability shapes downstream policy performance. Across 240 hours of offline demonstrations and over 3,000 online rollout trajectories, our extensive experiments show that downstream performance is strongly associated with value reliability. Reliable value functions provide better action-quality estimates, allowing value-guided offline RL to scale more effectively than quality-agnostic behavior cloning, and stabilize online improvement by prioritizing high-quality rollout data. Integrating reliable value guidance through offline pretraining with online improvement, our system achieves 86% success on millimeter-level precise chip insertion and 84% on generalizable block disassembly. We hope these findings highlight the importance of value-guided data utilization for effective policy improvement from heterogeneous robotic experience.

</details>


### [204] [Diffusion for Long-Horizon Multi-Robot Path Planning in Human-Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09911)
*Vaibhav Sanjay,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了MRRD框架，结合滚动时域、并行扩散推理和冲突搜索，实现了大规模机器人团队在密集人群中的实时、长时导航，支持15台机器人实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的行人路径固定时长且计算延迟高，难以适应不同目标距离和实时部署需求。

Method: 结合滚动时域方案（适应人类运动预测的有限范围）、并行扩散推理（可扩展生成类人路径）、冲突搜索机制（解决机器人间碰撞），以及基于紧迫性的时序条件（生成变速路径）和差异化引导项（兼顾社会意识和协调效率）。

Result: 在拥挤环境中成功扩展到15台机器人实时运行，在安全性和任务成功率上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: MRRD框架有效解决了多机器人路径规划中的实时性与社会适应性挑战，具备良好的可扩展性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Multi-robot path planning in human-shared environments requires a delicate balance between robust inter-robot coordination and socially aware behavior. While diffusion models excel at generating predictable, human-like paths, existing generative planners are often restricted to paths of fixed duration and high computational latency, limiting their adaptability to varying goal distances and hindering real-time deployment. We present Multi-Robot Rolling Diffusion (MRRD), a novel framework that enables real-time, long-horizon navigation for large robot teams through dense crowds. MRRD combines a rolling-horizon scheme to accommodate the limited prediction horizon of human motion, parallelized diffusion inference for scalable generation of human-like paths, and a conflict-based-search mechanism for resolving inter-robot collisions. It further incorporates urgency-based temporal conditioning to generate paths with varying speeds and employs differentiated guidance terms to maximize both social awareness around humans and efficient coordination between robots. Experimental results in crowded environments demonstrate that MRRD successfully scales to 15 robots in real-time, significantly outperforming existing baselines in both safety and mission success rates.

</details>


### [205] [A Numerically-Robust ROS 2 Port of iG-LIO: Diagnosing and Fixing Toolchain-Induced Failures in Incremental GICP LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09947)
*Afonso E. Carvalho,David Portugal,Paulo Peixoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了iG-LIO系统从ROS 1到ROS 2 Jazzy的移植过程，重点诊断了移植后出现的、由现代ROS 2工具链引发的数值崩溃问题（QoS不匹配导致IMU数据丢失/乱序，以及oneTBB+Eigen组合导致的未初始化累加器），并修复了Ouster点云解析、新增Velodyne Velarray M1600和Livox传感器支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将iG-LIO从ROS 1移植到ROS 2，并解决移植后由环境因素（非算法本身）导致的数值发散问题（NaN）。

Method: 对原始iG-LIO算法数学保持不变进行ROS 2移植，诊断并修复了QoS配置导致的IMU数据异常、oneTBB+Eigen未初始化累加器问题，修正了Ouster点云解析，新增多种传感器支持。

Result: 在Ouster OS0 Rev7、Ouster OS1 Rev7和Livox MID-360上验证成功，解决了移植后的数值崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是ROS 2移植报告和技术诊断，而非算法创新，为后续类似移植工作提供了可引用的参考。

Abstract: iG-LIO is a tightly-coupled LiDAR-inertial odometry system fusing generalized-ICP and point-to-plane constraints in an iterated error-state Kalman filter over an incremental voxel map. We report an open-source ROS 2 Jazzy port of the original ROS 1 implementation and, more importantly, the diagnosis of environment-induced numerical failures that appear only after the port: a mechanically faithful migration -- estimation mathematics left unchanged -- compiled and ran, yet diverged with NaN internal values. Both causes trace to the modern ROS 2 toolchain, not the algorithm: a Quality-of-Service (QoS) mismatch that silently drops and reorders IMU samples, and an uninitialized parallel-reduce accumulator arising from the oneTBB + Eigen combination shipped with current distributions. We further correct Ouster point-field parsing to ensure correct point cloud undistortion with newer Ouster revisions, add Velodyne Velarray M1600 support, provide both a compile-time-gated Livox CustomMsg path and a driver-free path for Livox sensors publishing standard PointCloud2 (e.g. Mid-360), and expose the runtime via YAML. The result has been validated in an Ouster OS0 Rev7, an Ouster OS1 Rev 7, and a Livox MID-360. This report is a citable reference for the port itself, not a claim on the underlying algorithm [1]. The ROS 2 port of iG-LIO described in this document can be found at https://github.com/Forestry-Robotics-UC/ig_lio/tree/ros2-jazzy.

</details>


### [206] [SEAMLiS: Visibility-Aware Safety for Perception-Limited Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09959)
*Taekyung Kim,Rahul H Kumar,Aswin D. Menon,Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEAMLiS框架，通过在执行层添加感知感知的姿态和位置滤波器，在有限传感范围内实现多机器人安全探索，避免因感知受限导致的碰撞风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主探索方法将探索规划与安全控制分离，在有限感知范围下，探索栈可能乐观地规划穿过未知空间，将传感器转向信息增益方向而非运动方向，导致障碍物检测过晚而无法及时避障。

Method: 提出SEAMLiS模块化执行层安全框架，包含：(1) 基于门控的姿态滤波器，在可见性提升策略与速度跟踪备份策略间切换；(2) 基于控制障碍函数(CBF)的位置滤波器，用于避开已知障碍物、新检测障碍物及其他机器人。

Result: 在随机模拟、Isaac Sim仿真及Crazyflie硬件实验中验证，单/多机器人场景下均实现无碰撞探索，同时保持可见性控制的大部分效率。

Conclusion: SEAMLiS能够在保留上游探索栈的前提下，通过执行层安全滤波有效解决有限传感下的探索安全问题，提供了充分的碰撞避免条件保证。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration in unknown environments is typically driven by informative frontiers, viewpoints, or trajectories, while local safety controllers avoid obstacles represented in the current map. Under finite sensing range and limited field of view, this separation can be unsafe: an exploration stack may plan optimistically through unobserved space and steer the sensor toward information gain rather than along the direction of motion, causing hidden obstacles to be detected too late for bounded-actuation avoidance. This paper presents SEAMLiS (Safe Exploration for Autonomous Multi-Robot Systems Under Limited Sensing), a modular execution-layer safety framework for decentralized multi-robot exploration. SEAMLiS preserves the upstream exploration stack, including the goal allocator and local planner, and enforces safety at the execution layer through perception-aware attitude and positional filters. A gatekeeper-based attitude filter switches between a visibility-promoting yaw policy and a velocity-tracking backup policy to preserve visibility of the critical known-free/unknown boundary with sufficient braking margin. A Control Barrier Function (CBF)-based positional filter then avoids known obstacles, newly detected obstacles, and other robots. We provide sufficient collision-avoidance conditions and validate the framework in randomized simulation, Isaac Sim, and Crazyflie hardware experiments. Results show collision-free exploration across tested single- and multi-robot settings while retaining much of the efficiency of visibility-promoting yaw control.

</details>


### [207] [Task Planning for Mobile Manipulation in Retail Stores using Foundation Models with Iterative Re-planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09962)
*Vismay Vakharia,Sanjana Garai,Rolif Lima,Nijil George,Vighnesh Vatsal,Kaushik Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)和视觉-语言模型(VLM)的任务规划方法，用于解决零售场景（如超市）中的补货问题，并在定制的全向移动操作平台上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售、仓储和物流行业的自动化可以通过提高吞吐量、降低成本以及缓解劳动力短缺带来的干扰来创造机会。以往的工作主要集中于结构化环境中的后端操作（如包装和分拣），而随着机器人移动操作硬件和基础模型的进步，自动化现在可以应用于更可变、以人为中心的环境（如零售货架）。本文旨在解决零售场景中的补货问题。

Method: 提出了一种使用大语言模型(LLM)和视觉-语言模型(VLM)的任务规划方法，结合用户驱动的提示和基于反馈的迭代重规划方法进行错误纠正。该系统部署在一个定制的全向移动操作平台上，并在PyBullet仿真环境中对拾放任务进行了端到端验证。

Result: 该系统在PyBullet仿真环境中对拾放任务进行了端到端验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过LLM和VLM的结合，以及迭代重规划机制，该方法能够有效地应对零售货架这类可变、以人为中心的环境中的自动化补货挑战。

Abstract: Automation in industries such as retail, warehousing and logistics presents opportunities for greater throughput, cost reduction and mitigation of disruptions from labour shortages. Previously, such efforts have focused on back-room operations involving packing and sorting in relatively structured environments. With advances in robotic mobile manipulation hardware and foundation models, automation can now be applied to more variable and human-centric environments such as retail store shelves. In this work, we present a task-planning approach using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to address the restocking problem in retail scenarios such as supermarkets. We demonstrate this system on a custom omnidirectional mobile manipulation platform, with user-driven prompts and a feedback-based iterative re-planning approach for error correction. The end-to-end system is validated in a PyBullet simulation environment for pick-and-place tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Chalito: An Extensible Library for Filtering-Based State Estimation in Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09968)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Chalito，一个用于四足机器人滤波状态估计算法基准测试的开源MATLAB/Python库，支持URDF模型导入、多种滤波方法，可在仿真和真实数据集上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人状态估计器实现常绑定特定机器人或软件栈，缺乏通用基准测试框架，阻碍了可重复性和算法创新。

Method: 开发Chalito库：直接从URDF导入机器人模型，支持多种滤波方法，设计为易扩展架构，兼容仿真与真实数据集。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个专门用于四足机器人滤波算法基准测试的开源库。

Conclusion: Chalito为四足机器人状态估计算法提供了统一的公平比较平台，有望推动该领域的可重复研究与算法进步。

Abstract: State estimation is essential for quadruped robots, enabling robust locomotion, navigation, and control. While many estimators have been proposed in the literature, existing implementations are often tied to specific robots or software stacks, making fair comparisons difficult. This lack of a general-purpose benchmarking framework hinders reproducibility and slows down algorithmic innovation. In this paper, we introduce Chalito, an extensible MATLAB/Python library for benchmarking filter-based state estimation algorithms in quadruped robots. Chalito imports robot models directly from URDF, supports multiple filtering approaches, and is designed to be easily extended with new methods. The framework runs on both simulated and real datasets, enabling systematic evaluation across robots and filters. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first open-source library exclusively dedicated to benchmarking filtering algorithms for quadruped robots.

</details>


### [209] [PinFT: Miniature 5-Axis Force/Torque Sensor Embeddable to Tweezer-like Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10000)
*Leo King,Jenny Chen,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PinFT，一种微型五轴电容式力/力矩传感器，可直接集成到镊子尖端。采用三PCB堆叠结构、分段镀通孔电极和硅弹性体介电层，通过2mm不锈钢针的位移实现五自由度力/力矩传感。实验表明，该传感器在抓取SMD电容、拔模拟毛发、撕裂硅胶样品等精细操作中，能够可靠区分操作成功与失败事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平行夹爪缺乏对每个夹爪尖端的直接力传感能力，难以在精细操作任务中有效感知和区分成功与失败事件（如滑移、物体弹出）。因此需要一种微型、可集成到镊子尖端的力/力矩传感器。

Method: 设计了三PCB堆叠的电容式传感器，中间采用硅弹性体介电层，通过2mm不锈钢针的位移实现五轴力/力矩检测。使用高阶多项式映射进行校准，并将传感器集成到3D打印镊子中，安装在平行夹爪上。

Result: 校准后力传感器平均绝对误差约0.23N，力矩误差约2.5mN·m，各轴R²均超过0.97。在SMD电容抓取、模拟毛发拔出、硅胶撕裂三个任务中，基于内部抓取力和净相互作用力的梯度特征，成功区分了操作成功与失败事件。

Conclusion: 直接在每个镊尖进行力传感，能够使标准平行夹爪在手持镊子的精细操作中有效监测和解读操作状态，为精细抓取操作提供可靠的力反馈。

Abstract: We present PinFT, a miniature five-axis capacitive force/torque sensor designed for direct tip-level integration into tweezer-like tools. The sensor employs a compact three-PCB stack with segmented plated through-hole electrodes and a silicone elastomer dielectric, enabling five-degree-of-freedom force and torque sensing ($F_x$, $F_y$, $F_z$, $T_x$, $T_y$) through displacement of a central 2\,mm-diameter stainless steel pin. The fabricated prototype was calibrated using a higher-order polynomial mapping, yielding mean absolute errors of approximately 0.23\,N for forces and 2.5\,mN$\cdot$m for torques, with coefficients of determination ($R^2$) exceeding 0.97 across all axes. To demonstrate practical utility, a 3D-printed tweezer integrating PinFT sensors at both tips was mounted on a parallel-jaw gripper and evaluated across three representative manipulation tasks: grasping a sub-millimeter SMD capacitor, pulling a simulated hair from a silicone substrate, and tearing a compliant silicone specimen. In all cases, per-tip force sensing reliably captured characteristic force signatures that distinguish successful manipulation from failure events -- including slip and object ejection -- using gradient-based features derived from internal grasp force and net interaction force. These results demonstrate that direct, per-tip force sensing enables standard parallel-jaw grippers to monitor and interpret fine manipulation tasks performed through a handheld tweezer.

</details>


### [210] [Runtime Safety Filtering for Learned Small UAS Separation Policies under GNSS Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10014)
*Alex Zongo,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在GNSS信号降级环境下，对比了两种保障小型无人机系统安全间隔的架构：动作过滤（通过控制屏障函数约束策略输出）和观察过滤（向策略输入最坏情况状态）。实验表明，动作过滤几乎不提升安全性，而观察过滤能将近距冲突减少90%，且对屏障函数的权衡参数鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的无人机分离保障在仿真中表现优异，但依赖准确的GNSS定位信息，而城市环境中多径、信号遮挡和干扰会严重降级导航完整性。因此需要研究在GNSS降级下如何部署学习型分离策略，核心架构问题在于：运行时安全机制应该过滤策略的动作还是其观测？

Method: 两种架构均先估计与有界观测不确定性一致的最坏情况交通状态，然后分岔：动作过滤通过离散时间控制屏障函数在最坏状态处约束策略输出；观察过滤则直接将最坏状态作为修正后的输入呈现给策略。

Result: 动作过滤几乎不提升安全性，而观察过滤将近距冲突减少90%，并且对屏障函数中分离距离和接近速率之间的权衡参数具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对于具有学习型安全行为的策略，保留策略的决策权优于用人工设计的约束覆盖其动作。观察过滤架构更有效，因为它让策略基于校正后的信息自主决策。

Abstract: Learning-based separation assurance for small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) achieves near-zero collision rates in simulation, but assumes accurate position and velocity information from Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS). This assumption fails in urban environments, where multipath propagation, signal blockage, and intentional interference degrade navigation integrity. This raises a fundamental architectural question for deploying learned separation policies under GNSS degradation: should runtime safety mechanisms filter the policy's actions or its observations? This work evaluates both approaches for multi-agent sUAS separation under adversarial GNSS degradation. Both architectures first estimate a worst-case traffic state consistent with bounded observation uncertainty, then diverge: action filtering constrains policy outputs via discrete-time control barrier functions evaluated at the worst-case state, while observation filtering presents the worst-case state directly to the policy as corrected input. Experimental results show that action filtering provides negligible safety improvement, while observation filtering reduces near mid-air collisions by 90% and remains robust to the barrier function's tradeoff between separation distance and closing rate. These results suggest that, for policies with learned safety behaviors, preserving the policy's decision authority outperforms overriding its actions with hand-designed constraints.

</details>


### [211] [Plug-and-Play Reweighting for Resilient Collaborative Decision-Making in Connected Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10037)
*Jiewen Liu,Rui Liu,Matthew Lee,Ming C. Lin,Xiaorui Liu,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RCDM框架，通过注意力机制和可插拔的重加权模块，在不重新训练的情况下有效抵御多机器人协同决策中的感知噪声和攻击，性能提升高达26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人协同决策方法对感知噪声和对抗攻击的鲁棒性不足，且通常需要针对特定攻击重新训练或依赖过于严格的扰动假设，缺乏实用性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出RCDM框架，包含基于注意力的编码器（提取单机器人感知嵌入）和基于注意力的解码器（融合协作感知并做出决策）。核心创新是设计了一个无需额外训练的可插拔重加权模块，通过分析邻域点一致性相对于局部结构，为偏离局部中位数的点分配更小权重，从而降低被污染观测的影响。

Result: 在高保真仿真中，考虑感知噪声和五种攻击类型，在多种事故易发场景下，RCDM相比现有方法性能提升最高达26%，达到最先进的鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: RCDM框架通过轻量级可插拔模块有效提升了多机器人协同决策的韧性，无需重新训练即可应对多种攻击，具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: Collaborative decision-making is a fundamental capability in multi-robot systems, such as connected autonomous vehicles. However, perceptual noise and adversarial attacks in collaborators can severely affect decision reliability. Overall, existing methods typically rely on retraining with attack-specific defenses or on restrictive perturbation assumptions to improve resilience, which limits their practicality. In this paper, we propose a novel Resilient Collaborative Decision-Making (RCDM) framework that consists of an attention-based encoder for extracting individual robot perceptual embeddings and an attention-based decoder for fusing collaborator perceptions and making decisions. To improve resilience to corrupted observations, we design a novel plug-and-play reweighting module that down-weights the influence of corrupted inputs by analyzing the consistency of neighborhood points relative to the local structure and assigning smaller weights to points that deviate strongly from the local median. This module can be seamlessly integrated into attention-based collaborative decision-making without requiring additional training. We evaluate our method in high-fidelity simulations, considering perceptual noise and five types of attacks across diverse accident-prone scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods by up to 26% and achieves state-of-the-art resilient performance.

</details>


### [212] [Direct Rotor Thrust Sensing and Feedback Control for Disturbance Rejection of Multirotors Using Load-cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10099)
*Peter Böhm,Michael Brünig,Peyman Z. Moghadam,Pauline E. I. Pounds*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过直接在旋翼处安装力传感器（load-cells）测量瞬时推力，并配合高速控制，以更快速、准确地抵抗多旋翼飞行器受到的气动干扰（如阵风、动态垂直入流、地面效应），实验证明了其可行性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多旋翼飞行器大多依赖间接方法（如从飞行器运动反推干扰力）来抵御气动扰动，这种方式存在滞后，即必须等到飞行轨迹受影响后才能补偿。尽管过去15-20年有多种方法试图加快干扰抑制速度，但仍有挑战。

Method: 在旋翼与机身之间安装力传感器（load-cells），直接测量旋翼产生的瞬时力，并以此作为反馈实现高速推力跟踪控制。硬件使用低成本商用组件搭建，在单/双旋翼跷跷板模型及实际飞行器上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，尽管力传感器的信号此前被认为噪声过大难以使用，但本文在多种复杂气动现象（阵风、动态垂直入流、地面效应）下，该方法均有效可行，能够快速准确地跟踪期望推力。

Conclusion: 直接力测量结合高速控制是一种可行且有效的抗扰动方案，能够克服传统间接方法的滞后问题，为多旋翼飞行器在复杂气动环境下的精确控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Gust disturbances, dynamic vertical inflow and ground effect are key adverse aerodynamic phenomena that induce variations in the forces acting on a multirotor and complicate its flight control. Miniature rotorcraft typically rely on simplified modelling of such effects to compute adjustments in thrust to counteract these forces. In the most basic case, disturbance force estimations are derived from the aircraft's motion and the generated thrust is assumed to exactly match that requested by the controller. However, such systems rely on the aircraft's trajectory to be affected before disturbances can be sensed and compensated. Numerous approaches presented over the last 15-20 years aim to reject external disturbances more quickly, but challenges remain.
  This paper presents a new approach in this category by measuring the instantaneous force of the rotors directly at the point of generation using load-cells and implementing high-speed control to accurately track the desired thrust. Measurements from load-cells were previously considered too noisy to provide meaningful input, but the experiments presented in the paper using purpose-built hardware from low-cost commodity components in single- and dual rotor see-saw models and a flying aircraft demonstrate both the feasibility and the effectiveness of the approach in the presence of complex aerodynamic phenomena.

</details>


### [213] [TAC-LOCO: Unified Whole-Body Control for Quadrupedal TACtile-Informed LOCO-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10132)
*Muqun Hu,Yuhao Zhou,Kabir Ray Malik,Chi Lin,Won Suk Lee,Yu She,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TAC-LOCO框架，将触觉传感器信息与本体感知融合，通过强化学习统一控制四足机器人的腿、臂和夹爪，在动态运动操作中实现抓取力降低47%，物体掉落率低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态全身控制方法缺乏触觉反馈，通常采用刚性抓取而非根据交互力自适应调节抓取力，导致在未知外力下难以维持稳定抓取。

Method: 提出TAC-LOCO统一强化学习框架，将柔性夹爪的触觉阵列观测编码为紧凑潜在表示，与本体感知融合，设计抓取稳定性奖励函数，联合控制腿部、手臂和夹爪。

Result: 在Unitree Go2机器人上零样本部署，实现动态触觉感知运动操作，抓取力降低47%，物体掉落率低于1%。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈能够有效提升动态全身控制中抓取的自适应能力和稳定性，为机器人在未知交互环境下进行运动操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamic loco-manipulation requires legged robots to coordinate whole-body motion while maintaining stable physical interaction with grasped objects under uncertain external forces. While tactile sensing has been widely studied for robotic manipulation, its role in dynamic whole-body control remains largely unexplored. Existing works without tactile feedback commonly grasp firmly rather than regulate the grasp according to the interaction. We propose TAC-LOCO, a tactile-augmented unified reinforcement learning framework that encodes tactile array observations from compliant grippers into a compact latent representation and joins it with proprioception for unified control of the legs, arm, and gripper. With effective grasp stability reward design, the policy learns to simultaneously track body velocity and end-effector trajectories, moderate grasp force, and prevent object slip under both gradual load changes and sudden release events. We deploy the policy zero-shot on a Unitree Go2 with an Interbotix WidowX 250 arm and tactile gripper, demonstrating dynamic tactile-informed loco-manipulation under varying external interactions, achieving a 47% reduction in grasping force and an object drop rate of less than 1%.

</details>


### [214] [Millimeter Wave Radar: From Synthetic Aperture to Probabilistic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10161)
*Jui-Te Huang,Ruoyang Xu,Michael Kaess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套从毫米波雷达到概率占用地图的完整流程，结合SAR处理与概率建模，在室内环境中验证了其有效性，并开源了数据集与GPU加速处理工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传感器在烟雾、雾等恶劣条件下失效，而毫米波雷达虽然能在这些条件下工作，但因其稀疏和噪声特性难以构建精确的概率地图。作者旨在解决这一矛盾，建立完整的雷达信号到概率地图的映射流程。

Method: 建立从原始雷达信号到概率占用地图的完整流程，包含两个核心步骤：合成孔径雷达（SAR）信号处理，以及后续的概率建模步骤。

Result: 在室内环境中进行了大量验证，比较了不同信号处理与概率建模方法，并通过下游路径规划任务评估了建图质量。同时分析了关键参数和天线阵列配置的影响。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明基于SAR的概率映射在真实机器人部署中既有有效性也有局限性。作者开源了级联毫米波雷达数据集和GPU加速信号处理流程以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Robust probabilistic mapping is essential for autonomous robotic systems operating in challenging environments. While traditional sensors fail in adverse conditions such as smoke and fog, millimeter wave (mmWave) radar sensors offer reliable sensing in such conditions. However, creating accurate probabilistic maps from radar data presents significant challenges due to the inherently sparse and noisy characteristics of radio wave measurements and signal processing steps. In an attempt to address these issues, we establish a complete pipeline from raw radar signals to probabilistic occupancy maps, incorporating Synthetic Aperture Radar processing followed by a probabilistic modeling step. We conduct extensive validation across indoor environments, comparing our approach against different signal processing and probabilistic modeling approaches. We also evaluate mapping quality through downstream path planning performance analysis. Furthermore, we investigate the impact of key parameters and antenna array configuration on mapping performance. The experimental results demonstrate both the effectiveness and limitations of SAR-based probabilistic mapping for real-world robotic deployment. To facilitate future research and broader adoption, we contribute an open-source cascaded mmWave radar dataset with an accompanying GPU-accelerated signal processing pipeline available at https://github.com/rpl-cmu/rpm.

</details>


### [215] [From Non-Rigid to Rigid: Safe Acquisition of Rigid Communication Graphs under Limited Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10170)
*Saharsh,Vedhas Talnikar,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限感知范围和动态条件下，无需初始刚性图即可实现多机器人系统无碰撞、时变刚性通信图的方法，并基于分层二阶一致性开发了分布式二次优化控制器，通过仿真和硬件实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信图刚性是多机器人编队控制的基础要求，但在实际中由于传感器感知范围有限和动态操作条件，确保并维持刚性通信拓扑具有挑战性，现有方法通常假设初始刚性图存在，缺乏在非理想条件下的刚性实现方案。

Method: 提出了一种分布式二次优化控制器，基于领导者-跟随者架构和分层二阶一致性机制；通信链路根据有限感知距离动态建立或断开；无需假设初始刚性图；跟随者无需任何智能体的全局绝对位置信息。

Result: 通过仿真和在运动捕捉环境中的硬件实验验证了该方法在有限感知能力下的可靠性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现异构非线性多机器人系统在有限感知范围下的无碰撞刚性时变通信图，且计算轻量、分布式，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Communication graph rigidity is a fundamental requirement in many multi robot formation control approaches. However, ensuring and maintaining a rigid communication topology becomes challenging in practice due to limited sensing ranges and dynamic operating conditions. This paper provides a method for achieving an inter robot collision free, rigid time varying communication graph, where communication links are established or broken according to limited sensing ranges, without assuming an initial rigid graph. In addition, the proposed approach guarantees the realization of a rigid graph for heterogeneous nonlinear multi robot systems. A computationally lean, distributed quadratic optimization-based controller is developed for a leader follower architecture, acquiring rigidity based on hierarchical second-order consensus among robots. Follower agents do not require global absolute positions of any agent, including their own. The proposed method is validated through both simulations and hardware experiments in a motion-capture environment, demonstrating reliable performance under the limited sensing capabilities of individual robots.

</details>


### [216] [On the Efficiency of LoRA Fine-Tuning for Vision-Language-Action Models in Industrial Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10172)
*Finn Ferchau,Daniel Pommer,Cristian Axenie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了低秩适配（LoRA）在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型π₀上的微调效果，发现秩为32的LoRA配合完整视觉编码器微调，性能与全微调无显著差异，但显存从36.2 GiB降至10.8 GiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将十亿参数级的VLA模型部署到工业硬件上需要微调以弥合具身差距，但全微调需要数据中心级GPU资源，成本高昂。作者希望找到更高效的微调方法。

Method: 对π₀（基于流匹配的VLA模型）进行LoRA微调的系统研究，在UR5e机械臂的四个精密装配任务上评估，扫描LoRA秩（r=8~256）、分配策略和组件冻结消融实验。

Result: LoRA在r=32时性能饱和；均匀分配至VLM骨干和动作专家即可；冻结VLM或限制视觉编码器仅用LoRA会显著降低性能；r=32 LoRA结合完整视觉编码器微调，峰值显存从36.2 GiB降至10.8 GiB，性能无损失。

Conclusion: LoRA（r=32）配合完整视觉编码器微调是一种实用的VLA模型部署方案，显著降低资源需求而不损失性能。具身适配需要语义和视觉双方面的可塑性。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on industrial hardware requires fine-tuning to bridge the embodiment gap. Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) provides maximal plasticity but requires data centre-grade GPUs. We present a systematic study of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for $π_0$, a flow-matching VLA, evaluated on four precision assembly tasks with a UR5e robotic manipulator. Across a sweep of LoRA ranks (r=8 to 256), allocation strategies, and component-freezing ablations, we find no statistically significant advantage of FFT over certain LoRA configurations. Performance saturates at r=32, and uniform allocation across the Vision-Language-Model (VLM) backbone and action expert proves sufficient. Freezing the VLM or restricting the vision encoder to LoRA significantly degrades performance, indicating that embodiment adaptation requires both semantic and visual plasticity. These results suggest that LoRA at r=32 with full vision encoder fine-tuning is a practical approach, reducing static peak VRAM from 36.2 to 10.8 GiB (parameters and optimizer states, activation memory excluded) without detectable performance loss.

</details>


### [217] [ActiveFly-Bench: Aligning Embodied Question Answering with Vision-Language-Action for Aerial Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10180)
*Weichen Zhang,Shiquan Yu,Yinan Zhu,Peizhi Tang,Shilong Ji,Zhiyuan Deng,Tianyi Lyu,Haoyang Wang,Xin Zeng,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveFly-Bench，首个连接网络空间推理与物理世界交互的无人机具身感知基准，包含三个层次任务（Air-EQA、OBP、FLUC），并开发了闭环无人机智能体ActiveFly。实验表明现有模型在主动感知的行为规划、视角调整等方面仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机缺乏将高层任务理解与物理世界主动感知相连接的系统性基准，亟需一个能桥接网络空间推理与物理世界交互的测试平台。

Method: 将主动感知分解为三个层次任务：空中具身问答（Air-EQA）、观察行为规划（OBP）和细粒度语言引导的无人机控制（FLUC）；从真实与模拟室外环境收集数据集；开发闭环无人机智能体ActiveFly，集成视觉-语言推理与细粒度控制。

Result: 在物理无人机平台部署并测试了代表性VLM和VLA模型，发现当前无人机智能体在行为规划、视角调整和鲁棒任务完成方面仍表现不佳。

Conclusion: ActiveFly-Bench为空中具身智能建立了新的测试基准，揭示了现有模型在主动感知任务中的关键不足。

Abstract: We introduce ActiveFly-Bench, the first benchmark to bridge cyberspace reasoning and physical-world interaction for UAV embodied perception. The benchmark decomposes active perception into three hierarchical tasks: Aerial Embodied Question Answering (Air-EQA), Observation Behavior Planning (OBP), and Fine-grained Language-guided UAV Control (FLUC), explicitly connecting high-level task understanding, behavior planning, and low-level control. The datasets are collected from both real-world and simulated outdoor environments for training and evaluation. We further develop ActiveFly, a closed-loop UAV agent that integrates visual-language reasoning with fine-grained control, and deploy it on a physical UAV platform. Experiments with representative VLMs and VLA models show that current UAV agents still struggle with behavior planning, viewpoint adjustment, and robust task completion in active perception. These results establish ActiveFly-Bench as a new testbed for embodied aerial intelligence.

</details>


### [218] [Source-Lifted Flow Matching for Intervenable Multimodal Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10206)
*He Zhang,Ying Sun,Pengteng Li,Ziyang Chen,Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Rao,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO
