Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于比较的序数风险学习框架，通过从驾驶数据中提取成对比较（时间演变、事件对比、物理反事实扰动），直接学习碰撞相关的风险排序，无需帧级风险标签。在100-Car和SHRP2数据集上，该框架在预警精度和提前时间上优于传统基于代理指标的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于碰撞数据稀缺且缺乏帧级风险标签，现有驾驶风险评估方法常依赖代理目标，这些代理目标与真实碰撞风险的对齐不完美，无法准确反映驾驶交互的相对危险程度。

Method: 提出基于比较的序数风险学习框架，从三种事件结构驾驶数据中提取成对比较：（1）安全关键序列的时间演变；（2）危险与正常交互的事件级对比；（3）基于物理的反事实扰动。实现三种风险评分函数实例化：直接学习风险分数、对齐现有单代理/多代理风险模型。

Result: 在100-Car和SHRP2数据集上的主动碰撞预警任务中，该框架在高召回率风险判别、预警精度和预警提前时间上均优于代表性代理指标基线，且在分布内和分布外评估中均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为自动驾驶系统和安全关键驾驶交互提供更可靠的风险评估，对主动安全研究具有重要贡献。

Abstract: Real-time driving risk assessment provides an essential basis for proactive safety by identifying and quantifying the danger of ongoing road interactions before adverse outcomes occur. However, due to the scarcity of collision data and frame-level risk labels, existing driving risk assessment methods often rely on surrogate objectives, which may imperfectly align with true collision risk and not faithfully reflect the relative danger of driving interaction. This paper proposes a comparison-based ordinal risk learning framework that learns collision-relevant risk scores from pairwise supervision in driving data, directly modeling relative risk ordering without requiring numerical frame-level risk labels. We derive pairwise comparisons from three sources of event-structured driving data for such ordinal risk learning: temporal progression within safety-critical sequences, event-level contrast between dangerous and normal interactions, and physics-based counterfactual perturbations. On this basis, instantiations with three risk-scoring function parameterizations are implemented, including directly learning risk scores from comparison data, and aligning existing single or multiple surrogate-based risk models. The proposed framework is evaluated on the 100-Car and SHRP2 naturalistic driving datasets using a proactive collision warning task. Results show that the proposed framework improves high-recall risk discrimination, warning precision, and warning lead time over representative surrogate-based baselines across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution evaluations. These results suggest that the proposed framework can contribute to proactive safety research by providing more reliable risk assessment for automated driving systems and safety-critical driving interactions.

</details>


### [255] [Pix2Act: Image-Space Manipulation Policies with Equivariant Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11167)
*Haojie Huang,Linfeng Zhao,Haotian Liu,Zhang Ye,Si-Yuan Huang,Mingxi Jia,Boce Hu,Fangzhou Lin,Yu Qi,Dian Wang,Robin Walters,Robert Platt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pix2Act是一种模仿学习方法，通过在相机平面生成连续2D关键点轨迹并利用三角测量恢复末端执行器姿态，将3D操控转化为2D预测问题。该方法利用观测与动作在同一坐标空间的等变性进行数据增强，提升泛化能力，在多种任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将操控动作表示为2D轨迹的方法虽简洁可解释，但面临轨迹超出相机视野和精度有限的挑战。Pix2Act旨在解决这些问题，同时利用图像空间与动作空间的对齐来实现更好的泛化。

Method: 1) 在每个相机平面中生成连续的图像空间关键点轨迹；2) 通过三角测量无损恢复末端执行器姿态；3) 利用观测与动作在同一坐标空间的等变性，对每个相机图像及其图像空间动作进行联合旋转增强；4) 设计能融合多视角同时尊重各视角旋转的网络架构。

Result: 在多种模拟和真实世界的操控任务中，Pix2Act优于最先进的基线方法，并且在相机受到扰动时仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Pix2Act通过将3D操控重新表述为2D预测问题，并利用等变数据增强隐式扩大数据分布支持，学习不变的动作结构，从而提升了泛化能力和整体性能。

Abstract: Representing manipulation actions as 2D trajectories in the camera plane provides a compact and interpretable basis for learning complex 3D manipulation policies. However, it also creates challenges from out-of-frame trajectories and limited precision. We propose Pix2Act, an imitation learning method that addresses these challenges by generating continuous image-space keypoint trajectories in each camera plane and losslessly recovering end-effector poses via triangulation. This reformulates high-dimensional 3D control as a simpler, more learnable 2D prediction problem. Crucially, it aligns observations and actions in the same coordinate space, enabling equivariant transformations to jointly rotate individual camera images together with their image-space actions. We analyze the symmetry properties of this augmentation and design a network architecture that can fuse multiple camera views while respecting their per-view rotations. As a result, Pix2Act implicitly enlarges the support of the data distribution and learns invariant action structures across transformations, yielding improved generalization and overall performance. Across diverse simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, Pix2Act outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and remains robust under camera perturbations.

</details>


### [256] [GeoGS-SLAM: Online Monocular Reconstruction Using Gaussian Splatting with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11184)
*Ruilan Gao,Letian Jin,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoGS-SLAM是一种在线单目密集重建系统，结合3DGS地图表示与学习到的几何先验，通过粗到细的优化策略联合优化相机位姿和场景地图，并在室内外基准上取得了优于现有方法的渲染质量和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM方法通常需要外部深度传感器提供几何信息，而利用几何先验的方法在优化过程中丢弃RGB信息导致重建质量下降。GeoGS-SLAM旨在结合两者优势，实现无需外部深度传感器的实时高质量密集重建。

Method: 1) 使用前馈视觉几何模型预测相机和场景先验；2) 从RGB输入和几何先验中直接采样高斯原语扩展场景地图；3) 通过粗到细策略联合优化相机位姿和场景地图，同时最小化光度损失和几何损失；4) 引入在线回环检测和位姿图优化保证全局一致性。

Result: 在室内外基准测试中，GeoGS-SLAM在渲染质量和跟踪精度上均优于现有方法，同时保持在线实时性能。

Conclusion: 结合3DGS表示与几何先验的联合优化策略能有效提升SLAM系统的密集重建质量和定位精度，无需依赖外部深度传感器即可实现实时性能。

Abstract: SLAM methods based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated impressive tracking and mapping performance, but typically require additional geometric information from external depth sensors. Meanwhile, recent SLAM systems that leverage geometric priors from pre-trained feed-forward models enable real-time dense reconstruction, yet often discard original RGB information during optimization, thus degrading overall reconstruction quality. We present GeoGS-SLAM, an online monocular dense reconstruction system that combines the 3DGS-based map representation with learned geometric priors. Given uncalibrated RGB input, we first employ a feed-forward visual geometry model to predict camera and scene priors. The Gaussian scene map is then expanded by directly sampling Gaussian primitives from both RGB input and geometric priors. Camera poses and the scene map are jointly optimized through a coarse-to-fine strategy that minimizes both photometric and geometric losses. To ensure global consistency, we further incorporate online loop closure detection and pose graph optimization. Extensive experiments across indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate that GeoGS-SLAM achieves superior rendering quality and tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art methods while maintaining online real-time performance. Project page: https://rlgao.github.io/geogs_slam.

</details>


### [257] [Stop to Decide: Latency-Aware Proprioceptive Navigation Primitives for Mapping-Free Quadruped Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11204)
*Hanting Suo,Haonan Yan,Liang Wang,Aiguo Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在计算资源受限的四足机器人上，导航循环因与视觉管道共享Jetson Orin而降至约15 Hz，导致传统身体俯仰信号检测楼梯顶部的方法失效。提出一种“爬升-稳定”节奏模式，在约15 Hz下将过冲从约47%降至0%，并给出临界循环率约19 Hz的部署规则。整套系统完全机载、无地图、无学习，在检测课程中18/20成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在计算受限（如共享Jetson Orin）时导航循环速率远低于控制器设计速率（降至约15 Hz），导致传统基于身体俯仰信号的楼梯顶部到达检测方法失效——机器人仍在爬升时就错误声明到达，从而发生顶部边缘过冲。需要一种不依赖高循环率的鲁棒楼梯检测方法。

Method: 提出“爬升-稳定”节奏（climb-settle cadence）替代传统连续运动中的检测模式；使用逻辑剂量-响应模型（logistic dose-response model）以v/f为变量建模失败概率；在约30/20/15 Hz及40 Hz外样本测试验证；系统包含IMU、四足力传感器、三个1D距离传感器、一个线阵摄像头，组合线跟随、三段式90°转弯策略和楼梯遍历。

Result: 爬升-稳定节奏在所有循环率下过冲接近零（22/45 vs 1/45，Fisher p≈2.4e-7）；约15 Hz下7/15 vs 0/15；40 Hz外样本测试模型预测与观察一致（33%观察 vs 43%/22%预测）；三段式转弯接触率20/20 vs 14/20；出口航向误差1.56° vs 5.64°；全程检测课程18/20成功（90%）。

Conclusion: 在计算严重受限的四足机器人上，采用爬升-稳定节奏而非传统连续运动检测，可消除楼梯顶部过冲。结合逻辑模型给出临界循环率约19 Hz（0.30 m/s时）的部署规则。整套机载无地图无学习系统在楼宇检测任务中表现出高成功率（90%）。

Abstract: Compute-constrained quadrupeds often run their navigation loop far below the controller's design rate: sharing the onboard Jetson Orin with the vision pipeline slows our stair loop to about 15 Hz. This latency breaks a standard proprioceptive pattern: declaring stair-summit arrival from the body-pitch signal while still climbing. On a stepped platform whose 50 cm top is shorter than the robot (Unitree Go2, about 75 cm), in-motion detection overshoots the top edge with probability rising with the per-period advance v/f (the slowest about 15 Hz cell partly diluted by a separate non-arrival mode), whereas a climb-settle cadence holds overshoot near zero at every loop rate (pooled 22/45 vs 1/45 over about 30/20/15 Hz; Fisher p about 2.4e-7; 7/15 vs 0/15 at the deployed about 15 Hz). A logistic dose-response model in v/f captures the failure; a pre-specified 40 Hz out-of-sample test favours the protocol-clean fit (33% observed vs 43%/22% predicted), giving a deployment rule (critical loop rate about 19 Hz at 0.30 m/s). The detector sits in a fully onboard, mapping-free and learning-free stack: built-in inertial measurement unit, four foot-force channels, three 1-D ranges, one line camera, chaining line-following, a three-segment maneuver for 90-degree corners in a 55 cm corridor (20/20 contact-free vs 14/20 with 12 wall contacts for in-place yaw; exit-heading error 1.56 degrees vs 5.64 degrees), and stair traversal, completing the inspection course in 18/20 trials (90%). Results are from a single course geometry, platform, and operator.

</details>


### [258] [Towards Predictive, Aligned, and Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11270)
*Peijun Tang,Shangjin Xie,Baifu Huang,Binyan Sun,Haotian Yang,Kuncheng Luo,Weiqi Jin,Shilin Fang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lumo-2是一个潜在世界-动作模型，通过在潜在空间中对世界动态进行推理来生成动作，并采用多阶段模态预对齐策略，在机器人操作任务上超越了现有的VLA和WAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于重构的动作分词目标会导致表示偏向底层信号保真度，使得重建质量与下游控制性能之间出现错配，因此需要更好的表示对齐策略。

Method: 提出Lumo-2潜在世界-动作模型，包含：(1) 学习潜在世界动态以捕捉物理视觉转换；(2) 多阶段模态预对齐策略，逐步将动作表示与潜在世界动态、视觉和语言对齐；(3) 在潜在空间中实现预测推理。

Result: 在需要时间推理、物理理解或高控制复杂度的真实世界任务（包括长程和灵巧操作）上，Lumo-2一致优于强VLA和WAM基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的多模态对齐和预测推理是推进具身智能的基本原则。

Abstract: Learning, at its core, extends beyond memorization to the ability to reason and solve novel problems by navigating a space of possibilities. We introduce Lumo-2, a latent world-action model that generates actions by reasoning over world dynamics in latent space. The learned latent world dynamics capture physically grounded visual transitions, naturally encoding future possibilities and providing a unified substrate for cross-modal alignment. This formulation enables predictive reasoning akin to world modelling while remaining lightweight and focused on physical dynamics relevant to control. Central to our approach is the hypothesis that action generation quality is governed by the geometry of the latent space. We observe that standard reconstruction-based action tokenization objectives induce representations biased toward low-level signal fidelity, leading to misalignment between reconstruction quality and downstream control performance. To address this limitation, we propose a multi-stage modality pre-alignment strategy in which action representations are progressively aligned with latent world dynamics, vision, and language. This process enforces cross-modal consistency, promotes abstraction, and induces a structured latent space for predictive reasoning. We provide a systematic empirical study of latent world modelling and modality alignment, analyzing their roles in scaling laws and out-of-distribution generalization. Results show that Lumo-2 consistently outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action model (WAM) baselines, with gains on challenging real-world tasks requiring temporal reasoning, physical understanding, or high control complexity, including long-horizon and dexterous manipulation. These findings suggest that structured multimodal alignment and predictive reasoning are fundamental principles for advancing embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [259] [CR-Solver: GPU-Accelerated Kinematics Solver for Tendon-driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11340)
*Heqing Yang,Yang Yi,Linqing Zhong,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CR-Solver，一种基于GPU加速并行优化的两阶段运动规划求解器，用于肌腱驱动连续体机器人，将逆运动学、路径跟踪和轨迹规划统一在约束非线性优化框架中。在三个任务上，成功率>95%，精度达毫米级，相比传统CPU求解器显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人虽具备柔顺性、高灵活性和安全交互能力，但现有运动规划库大多基于刚体假设，缺乏针对连续体机器人的快速实用的运动规划工具。

Method: 提出两阶段优化求解器CR-Solver，将逆运动学、路径跟踪和轨迹规划统一为约束非线性优化问题，并利用GPU加速并行优化求解。

Result: 在三个任务上验证，成功率>95%，精度达毫米级，相比传统CPU求解器实现显著加速。

Conclusion: CR-Solver用纯Python实现，降低了采用门槛，为连续体机器人的高性能运动规划提供了实用、可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Continuum robots provide intrinsic compliance, high dexterity, and safe physical interaction, enabling navigation and manipulation in confined and unstructured environments. Despite recent advances in sensing and control, heightening the need for precise motion generation, most widely used planning libraries are grounded in rigid-body assumptions, creating a critical gap for fast and practical tools for continuum robots. To address this, we present CR-Solver, a two-stage, optimization-based solver for the motion generation of tendon-driven continuum robots. Our method unifies inverse kinematics, path following, and trajectory planning within a single constrained nonlinear optimization framework. Leveraging GPU-accelerated parallel optimization, CR-Solver delivers fast, accurate, and constraint-aware solutions. We validate our approach on three tasks, demonstrating significant speedups over traditional CPU-based solvers while achieving a consistently high success rate above 95% and millimeter-level accuracy. The solver is implemented in pure Python, reducing the barrier to adoption and offering a practical, extensible foundation for continuum robots' high-performance motion planning.

</details>


### [260] [A Glimpse into Long-term Physical Coexistence with Intelligent Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11377)
*Weiqi Jin,Peijun Tang,Kuncheng Luo,Baifu Huang,Binyan Sun,Haotian Yang,Shangjin Xie,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHILIA是一个基于机器人网关抽象构建的多机器人智能体系统，通过解耦高层语义推理与低层执行，支持多种用户界面、机器人形态和策略后端的即插即用集成，并在Astribot S1机器人上验证了其在长时域、灵巧服务任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期与智能机器人物理共存需要的不只是优秀的机器人策略，还需要支持多样化的用户界面、维护长时记忆、协调多机器人形态，并将人类意图转化为安全的物理执行。现有系统缺乏统一的抽象来解耦高层推理与低层执行，导致系统难以扩展和组合。

Method: 提出机器人网关抽象（Robot Gateway），统一暴露机器人本地运行时、感知、导航、语音和策略等能力；将低频高语义的智能体推理与高频低层的机器人执行解耦；支持用户界面、机器人形态和策略后端的即插即用集成。

Result: 在Astribot S1机器人上验证了架构有效性，展示了从简单整理到复杂长时域灵巧服务任务（如打包背包、提起垃圾袋）的代表性用例，强调了在人机交互流程中，上下文理解用户意图与偏好以及人在环确认/调整的必要性。

Conclusion: PHILIA通过机器人网关抽象实现了用户界面的组合性，使得用户界面、机器人形态、策略、导航或交互算法的进步可以独立改进整体体验而无需重新设计系统，为未来异构机器人平台的统一能力接口提供了设计契约。

Abstract: Long-term physical coexistence with intelligent robots requires more than capable robot policies. A persistent robotic assistant must support diverse user-facing interfaces, maintain long-horizon memory of people and preferences, coordinate across robot embodiments, and translate human intent into safe physical execution. We introduce PHILIA, a multi-robot agent built around a robot gateway abstraction. PHILIA retains the rich interaction and tool ecosystem of OpenClaw while exposing robot-local runtimes, onboard perception, navigation, speaker, and robot policies through a unified capability interface. This design decouples low-frequency, high-semantic agent reasoning from high-frequency, low-level robot execution, enabling plug-and-play integration of user interfaces, robot embodiments, and policy backends. As a result, the user experience becomes compositional: advances in user interfaces, robot embodiments, robot policies, navigation, or interaction algorithms can improve the overall experience without redesigning the system. We validate the architecture on Astribot S1 robots while designing the robot gateway contract to support future heterogeneous robot platforms through a shared capability interface for observation, task execution, navigation, speech playback, status monitoring, and task cancellation. We present representative use cases in which agent memory and scene understanding are grounded in robot actions. These span interactive household scenarios, ranging from simple organization to challenging long-horizon and dexterous service tasks, such as packing a backpack and lifting a garbage bag. We highlight the human-robot interaction flow, where contextual understanding of user intent and preferences, together with human-in-the-loop confirmation or adjustment during execution, is essential for effective assistance.

</details>


### [261] [From Sketch Prior to Trajectories: A Mission-Oriented Coordinated Navigation Framework for Indoor UAV Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11386)
*Xinhang Xu,Ruiyang Liu,Haotian Jin,Yi Wang,Hongming Shen,Jianping Li,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用草图先验的面向任务的无人机集群协同导航框架，通过拓扑对齐与分层2D-3D协调规划，在室内多房间/多楼层环境中实现任务导向的可通行性引导与轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内无人机任务（如巡检、安防、物流）往往是任务导向而非探索导向，需要按预定顺序访问任务相关区域。这类任务的区域级信息可从预部署的草图先验（如平面图、CAD布局）获取，且飞行高度相对固定，区域切换主要由平面连通性决定。基于这些观察，作者希望利用草图先验来指导多无人机的室内协同导航。

Method: 提出面向任务的协同导航框架：1) 利用机载观测进行拓扑对齐，将草图先验与实时感知融合；2) 构建面向任务的可通行性表征；3) 设计分层2D-3D协调导航架构——2D引导路径规划生成任务导向的引导路径，再通过引导驱动的3D轨迹优化生成动态可行且无碰撞的轨迹。

Result: 仿真与实物实验验证了该方法在结构化多房间室内环境中的有效性；在有/无通信条件下均展示了协同导航能力；多楼层仿真结果证明了系统在分层室内结构中的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 利用草图先验与分层规划的思想，可在通信受限的室内环境中实现高效、任务导向的多无人机协同导航，具有良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: UAV swarm for applications, such as indoor inspection, security patrol, and logistics delivery, are often mission-oriented rather than exploration-oriented. In these tasks, UAVs are required to visit task-relevant regions in a prescribed sequence, and such region-level mission information can often be obtained from pre-deployment sketch-map priors, such as floor plans, CAD layouts, or evacuation diagrams. Although these tasks are executed in three-dimensional space, UAVs usually fly within a specific altitude layer or a nearly fixed altitude range on each floor, making mission-level region transitions mainly governed by planar connectivity. Based on these observations, this paper proposes a mission-oriented coordinated navigation framework that exploits sketch-map priors for multi-UAV indoor operations. Onboard observations are used to perform topological alignment, and the aligned prior is fused with online observations to construct a mission-oriented traversability representation. A layered 2D--3D coordinated navigation framework is further developed, where 2D guided path planning generates mission-oriented guide paths and guide-driven 3D trajectory optimization produces dynamically feasible and collision-free trajectories. Simulation and real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in structured multi-room indoor environments and further demonstrate its coordinated navigation capability under both communication-available and communication-loss conditions. Multi-floor simulation results show the scalability of the system to layered indoor structures.

</details>


### [262] [WALA Learning Executable Latent Actions from Action-Labeled Demonstrations and Action-Free Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11397)
*Jiahao Liu,Zhongpu Xia,Shuai Tian,Huangrui Li,Yuhang Zheng,Ning Ma,Xin Fu,Xiaotian Liu,Jing Li,Yixian Li,ShangQing Zhou,Zebin Xing,Linbo Wang,Chaoyue Li,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WALA是一种从动作标注演示和动作自由视频中学习可执行潜在动作的框架，通过预测DINOv3特征空间和深度空间的未来变化来训练潜在动作模型，在多个基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略通常依赖昂贵的动作标注演示数据，难以规模化扩展；而大规模视频数据虽然包含丰富物理交互，但缺乏可执行的机器人动作标签。为了解决这一数据瓶颈，作者提出利用动作自由视频中的监督信号来辅助策略学习。

Method: 首先从视频中预训练语义-几何潜在动作模型，通过预测未来观测在DINOv3特征空间和深度空间中的变化来建模。策略训练时，预训练编码器提供稳定潜在动作目标，解码器作为可训练的潜在世界模型。视觉-语言骨干网络生成的潜在动作同时受机器人动作预测、潜在动作目标匹配和未来动态预测三个损失联合监督。

Result: 在RoboTwin上取得强性能，在RoboCasa上以75.2%平均成功率达到新SOTA，在真实机器人操作任务中提升了策略性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: WALA成功证明了动作自由视频可以通过语义-几何潜在动作建模的方式为机器人策略学习提供有效的动力学监督，缓解了对大量动作标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Generalizable robot policies typically rely on action-labeled robot demonstrations, which are expensive to collect and difficult to scale. In contrast, large-scale human and robot videos contain rich physical interactions but often lack executable robot action labels. We present WALA, a framework for learning executable latent actions from both action-labeled demonstrations and action-free videos. WALA first pretrains a semantic-geometric latent action model from videos by modeling the evolution between current observations and sparsely sampled future observations. Instead of reconstructing raw pixels, WALA predicts future deltas in the DINOv3 feature space and dense depth space, preserving task-relevant semantic and geometric structure while reducing sensitivity to appearance details. During policy training, the pretrained encoder provides stable latent action targets, and the decoder serves as a trainable latent world model. The latent actions generated by the vision-language backbone are jointly supervised by robot action prediction, latent action target matching, and future dynamics prediction. This enables action-labeled demonstrations to provide executable control supervision, while action-free videos contribute dynamics supervision without requiring robot action annotations. Experiments show that WALA achieves strong performance on RoboTwin, sets a new state-of-the-art result on RoboCasa with 75.2% average success, and improves both policy performance and generalization in real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [263] [EDAR: Learning Environment-Dependent Action Representations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11427)
*Yuecheng Xu,Tong Yang,Jingkai Jia,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EDAR是一种将动作表示同时基于可执行控制结构和预期视觉结果的方法，通过结合运动指令与环境条件效应，提升机器人操作中的策略学习效果，尤其是在长周期任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作表示方法仅编码动作流本身的结构，忽略了动作在不同场景下会产生不同结果这一事实，而操作的本质是改变环境，因此需要将动作语义与环境依赖的效果联系起来。

Method: 提出EDAR（Environment-Dependent Action Representation），将动作令牌同时锚定在可执行控制结构和预期的视觉结果上，通过耦合运动指令与其环境条件效应来学习具有交互语义的动作空间。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作基准测试中，EDAR显著提升了策略学习性能，特别是在长周期操作任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将动作表示同时基于可执行控制结构与环境条件视觉变化，对于学习有效的操作策略至关重要。

Abstract: Learning effective action representations is critical for robotic manipulation, where raw control trajectories are often noisy, redundant, and difficult to model directly. Existing methods mainly encode the structure of the action stream itself, treating the role of actions in the environment as implicit. Yet manipulation is about changing the world: the same action segment can induce different outcomes under different scene contexts, making action semantics inherently environment-dependent. We propose EDAR, an Environment-Dependent Action Representation that grounds action tokens in both executable control structure and expected visual consequences. By coupling motor commands with their environment-conditioned effects, EDAR encourages the learned action space to capture interaction semantics rather than merely command-level patterns. Experiments on simulated and real-robot manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that EDAR improves downstream policy learning, especially in long-horizon manipulation. These results highlight the importance of grounding action representations in executable control structure and environment-conditioned visual change.

</details>


### [264] [Towards Human-level Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11481)
*Puhao Li,Zeyuan Chen,Yingying Wu,Pengkun Wei,Yuyang Li,Tianyu Wang,Jiaxiao Shi,Mingrui Yu,Baoxiong Jia,Song-chun Zhu,Tengyu Liu,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TeleDexter，一种手-物协同追踪控制器，通过单阶段强化学习将从人类参考动作中提取的连续子目标映射为底层接触执行，实现了非逐帧轨迹模仿的多样化操作学习，在七项灵巧遥控操作任务中达到 75% 的平均成功率（所有基线均失败），并支持零样本迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥控操作系统远无法像人类一样通过动态手-物接触精准控制物体运动，缺乏在单手中自如操控工具、切换抓取和重新定位物体的灵巧操作能力。

Method: 手-物协同追踪控制器，基于人类参考运动中的连续协同追踪子目标进行训练，采用混合奖励（稀疏子目标目标+密集追踪奖励）实现多样化交互模态学习，仅需单阶段强化学习，结合随机动作掩码和领域随机化实现零样本迁移。

Result: 在七项挑战性灵巧遥控任务（包括物体重定向和长时程工具使用）上达到 75% 平均成功率，所有基线方法均完全失败；收集的示范数据通过行为克隆成功训练出自主策略。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着向人类级别灵巧遥控操作迈出了实质性一步，证明了基于子目标学习和混合奖励的强化学习范式在解决复杂手-物接触动力学问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Humans routinely wield tools, swap grasps, and reposition objects within a single hand, seamlessly orchestrating contact transitions that span translation, reorientation, and finger gaiting. Endowing robot dexterous hands with this level of in-hand dexterity through teleoperation requires precise control of object motion via dynamic hand-object contact, yet current teleoperation systems remain far from this capability. To bridge this gap, we take a major step towards human-level dexterous teleoperation by introducing TeleDexter, a hand-object co-tracking controller that maps operator intent into learned, low-level contact execution. The controller is trained on consecutive co-tracking subgoals derived from human reference motions, utilizing a hybrid reward that couples sparse subgoal objectives with dense tracking rewards to enable learning across diverse interaction modalities rather than frame-wise trajectory imitation. The entire pipeline requires only single-stage RL and, with random action masking and domain randomization, transfers zero-shot to the real robot. We evaluate TeleDexter on seven challenging dexterous teleoperation tasks spanning object reorientation and long-horizon tool use across two dexterous hands, achieving a 75% average success rate where all baselines consistently fail. Furthermore, the collected demonstrations successfully train autonomous policies via behavioral cloning, marking a concrete step towards human-level dexterous teleoperation.

</details>


### [265] [See like a Robot: Robot-Centric Pointmaps for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11498)
*Byungkun Lee,Dongyoon Hwang,Dongjin Kim,Hojoon Lee,Minho Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用机器人中心点云图(robot-centric pointmaps)解决VLA模型中的观测-动作坐标系不匹配问题，该方法在多种基线测试中表现优异，尤其当相机视角发生变化时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型预测的动作定义在机器人自身3D坐标系中，但场景观测通常在相机坐标系下进行，这种坐标系不匹配导致模型在跨视角泛化时性能下降。

Method: 提出机器人中心点云图(pointmaps)，将场景点在机器人坐标系中的3D坐标存储为图像像素值，在保留2D网格结构的同时提供机器人帧的3D几何信息，便于直接集成到现有2D VLA模型中。

Result: 在RoboCasa数据集上，pointmaps同时提升了pi0.5和SmolVLA的性能，并优于代表性的相机视角基线和3D感知基线；真实机器人实验中，在未见过的相机位姿下，pointmaps的优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 机器人中心点云图是一种简单有效的解决方案，能弥合观测与动作坐标系之间的差距，增强VLA模型的跨视角泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict robot actions from visual observations and language instructions. These actions are defined in the robot's own 3D coordinate frame, yet most VLAs observe the scene in the camera frame, creating a frame mismatch between where the scene is observed and where actions are defined. The mismatch is benign under a fixed viewpoint, where the policy can memorize a single observation-to-action mapping, but grows harder as large-scale datasets aggregate demonstrations across diverse camera setups and the policy must generalize this mapping across viewpoints. We address this mismatch with robot-centric pointmaps, images whose pixels store the 3D coordinates of scene points in the robot frame. Pointmaps provide robot-frame 3D geometry while preserving the dense H x W grid expected by pretrained 2D VLAs, so they integrate into existing VLAs with minimal architectural change. On RoboCasa, pointmaps improve both pi0.5 and SmolVLA and outperform representative camera-viewpoint and 3D-aware baselines. In real-robot experiments, their advantage over an RGB-only policy widens when the camera is moved to a placement unseen during training.

</details>


### [266] [ERR@HRI 3.0 Challenge: Multimodal Detection of Errors and Anticipation in Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11570)
*Maria Teresa Parreira,Micol Spitale,Maia Stiber,Shiye Cao,Amama Mahmood,Chien-Ming Huang,Hatice Gunes,Wendy Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ERR@HRI 3.0挑战赛提供了两个自然场景下的视频数据集（BAD和Bad Idea），用于开发机器人交互中的错误检测与预防方法。三个参赛团队提交的模型均超越了基线CNN，展示了多模态、可泛化错误检测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人错误检测方法多局限于特定场景或受控环境，缺乏泛化性和真实世界适用性。为突破这一瓶颈，ERR@HRI 3.0通过提供自然场景下的原始视频数据，推动端到端的错误检测与预防方法创新。

Method: 提供两个众包采集的自然场景数据集：(1) BAD数据集（45人观看机器人/人类失败反应的视频）；(2) Bad Idea数据集（29人预判动作结果的预期面部反应）。设置三个赛道：旁观者反应检测（Track 1）、预期结果预测（Track 2）、跨数据集泛化（Track 3）。参赛者开发多模态机器学习模型。

Result: 三支参赛团队提交的模型在各自任务上均超越了挑战赛提供的卷积神经网络（CNN）基线模型。

Conclusion: ERR@HRI 3.0验证了利用自然场景众包数据开发可泛化、上下文感知和预期性错误检测系统的可行性，为人机交互中错误处理的研究提供了基准数据集与任务框架。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into human environments, their ability to detect and respond to errors remains critical for maintaining user trust and interaction quality. While recent advances in machine learning have improved error detection capabilities, most approaches are limited to specific contexts, controlled settings, or pre-extracted features, limiting their generalizability and applicability to real-world conditions. To address this challenge, the third edition of the ERR@HRI Challenge (ERR@HRI 3.0) provided researchers with two complementary datasets that enable end-to-end innovation in methods for both detecting and preventing errors in human-robot interaction. The challenge offered raw, non-anonymized video data from naturalistic settings: (1) the Bystander Affect Detection (BAD) dataset, containing webcam recordings of 45 participants' spontaneous reactions to robot and human failure scenarios; and (2) the Bad Idea dataset, featuring 29 participants' anticipatory facial responses while predicting action outcomes before failures occur. Both datasets were collected via crowdsourcing, capturing the inherent variability of real-world conditions. This naturalistic variability, while challenging, provides an authentic testbed for developing robust error detection systems. Participants developed multimodal machine learning models for bystander reaction detection (Track 1) and anticipatory outcome prediction (Track 2), with an optional cross-dataset generalization track (Track 3). Three teams submitted valid models, all of which surpassed our convolutional neural network baselines. This paper describes the datasets, tasks, baselines, and results of ERR@HRI 3.0, and discusses implications for building generalizable, context-aware, and anticipatory error detection systems for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [267] [WarpMPC: Large-Batch MPC on GPU via ADMM with Unrolled $LDL^\top$ Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11603)
*Henrik Hose,Se Hwan Jeon,Charles Khazoom,Sangbae Kim,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对GPU的大批量SQP迭代数值优化方法，通过共享稀疏性、展开线性分解、优化内存布局等技术，在WarpMPC框架中实现了比基线快3-25倍的吞吐量，并可在4分钟内训练神经网络MPC近似器用于四旋翼飞行器控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大批量（10,000-100,000+）SQP迭代在GPU上的吞吐量瓶颈问题，尤其是ADMM求解器中稀疏线性分解与求解占主导运行时间的问题。

Method: 1) 利用批次内所有MPC问题共享相同的时间、成本、约束稀疏性；2) 展开稀疏线性因子分解与求解；3) 优化内存布局避免带宽瓶颈；4) 填充减少的展开分解分段策略；5) 依赖级别调度的反向求解加速灵敏度计算。

Result: 在非线性倒立摆、四旋翼和人形机器人基准上实现8,000-250,000 SQP迭代/秒的吞吐量，比基线快3-25倍；4分钟内可合成数据集并训练神经网络MPC近似器，在硬件实验中稳定控制纳米四旋翼。

Conclusion: 通过数值优化显著提升了GPU上大批量MPC问题的求解效率，验证了训练神经网络近似器的实用价值，在机器人实时控制中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: This paper introduces numerical optimizations for maximizing throughput on GPU when solving large batches (10,000 to over 100,000) of sequential quadratic programming (SQP) iterations, where all problems have the same structure. The optimizations are implemented in a toolbox WarpMPC for model-predictive control (MPC) in JAX and Warp. Based on the insight that all MPC problem instances in a batch share the same sparsity in time, cost, and constraints, we propose unrolling sparse linear factorizations and solves, which dominate alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) solver runtime. We avoid memory access bottlenecks and wasting computations via optimized memory layout, padding-reducing segmentation of the unrolled factorization, and dependency level scheduled backsolves, additionally accelerating sensitivity computation. We achieve throughputs of 8,000 to 250,000 SQP iterations per second on nonlinear cartpole, quadrotor, and humanoid robot benchmarks, outperforming baselines by 3$\times$ to 25$\times$. We illustrate practical usefulness by synthesizing a dataset and training a neural network approximation of an MPC in under 4 minutes that stabilizes a nano quadrotor in hardware experiments.

</details>


### [268] [SKooP: Symmetric Koopman Predictions for Faster and More Generalizable Legged Robot Locomotion with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11624)
*Evelyn D'Elia,Weishu Zhan,Giulio Turrisi,Giulio Romualdi,Giuseppe L'Erario,Raffaello Camoriano,Wei Pan,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SKooP方法，结合形态对称性与Koopman模型（通过自编码器学习），为强化学习中的评论家提供更平滑、信息更丰富的特权观测，同时在演员、评论家、编码器和解码器网络中引入群对称性，显著提升了四足机器人双足行走任务的样本效率和奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习算法样本效率低下；现有将物理先验编码到学习中的方法大多仅在低维基准系统上验证，缺乏对高维、复杂非线性动力学机器人的适用性。

Method: 1）利用自编码器学习Koopman模型（系统动力学的线性表示）；2）将Koopman预测作为评论家的特权观测，提供更平滑、信息更丰富的特征；3）在演员、评论家、编码器和解码器网络中均引入群对称性，实现高度等变的策略。

Result: 在四足机器人的多挑战性双足行走任务中，SKooP一致地减少了收敛时间并提高了学习奖励；学得的策略可迁移至不同仿真环境。

Conclusion: SKooP通过对称性与Koopman模型的结合，有效提升了高维机器人RL策略学习的样本效率和性能，且具有良好的迁移性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms classically suffer from poor sample efficiency. In robotics, a recent line of work has emerged addressing this problem by encoding physics priors in the learning process. However, most of these approaches are validated on well-defined, low-dimensional benchmark systems rather than high-dimensional robots with complex nonlinear dynamics. In this paper, we introduce \textit{SKooP (Symmetric Koopman Predictions)}, an approach combining the advantages of morphological symmetries with those of a Koopman model learned via autoencoder to enhance policy learning. SKooP learns a Koopman model of the system dynamics alongside the policy. The resulting Koopman predictions are used as privileged observations for the critic, allowing the agent to learn based on smoother, more informative features. We also incorporate group symmetries into the actor, critic, encoder and decoder networks to produce a highly equivariant policy. The SKooP approach is validated via in-depth analysis of the learned Koopman models and symmetric policies to showcase how each of these influences the agent's performance. We also show that the learned policies are transferable to different simulation environments. Our results show that SKooP consistently reduces convergence time and increases the learned reward for multiple challenging bipedal locomotion tasks on a quadruped robot. Project page: https://evelyd.github.io/SymmetricKoopmanPredictions/

</details>


### [269] [Breaking the 15% Barrier: A Real-World Data-Driven System for Proactive Social Robot Triggered by User Nonverbal Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11633)
*Yuga Yano,Yuki Okafuji,Ryo Miyoshi,Sanae Yamashita,Yoshiki Ohira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究零售服务机器人与顾客交互中的非语言行为（如接近、挥手、指物、展示物品），发现15.3%的机器人回应由非语言行为触发，并提出一种结合非语言线索令牌的LLM对话框架，实现无需手工规则的主动响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联语音管道（STT-LLM-TTS）仅处理语音输入，但在真实零售场景中，大量顾客-机器人交互由非语言行为（接近、挥手、指物、展示物品）发起或引导，音频-only系统存在明显局限。

Method: 1）在真实商店部署6天，收集并分析非语言触发的交互数据；2）定义一组频繁出现的服务相关非语言线索；3）开发实时多人物、多标签的非语言行为识别器；4）提出对话框架，将识别到的非语言线索令牌作为条件输入LLM生成响应，并在展示物品时可选使用视觉-语言模型。

Result: 15.3%的机器人话语由用户非语言行为触发而非语音输入；离线评估显示非语言触发轮次表现良好；在线原型实现了对用户非语言线索的实时响应。

Conclusion: 非语言行为在人机交互中不可忽视，将非语言线索融入LLM对话框架能显著增强服务机器人的交互能力，突破纯语音管道的局限性。

Abstract: Service robots in retail stores increasingly rely on cascaded speech pipelines (STT-LLM-TTS), yet many customer-robot interactions are initiated or guided by nonverbal behaviors such as approaching, waving, pointing, or showing items. This paper studies such cues in a real-world store deployment with a teleoperated humanoid robot and shows that a non-negligible portion of robot turns are triggered by nonverbal behaviors rather than spoken input, revealing a limitation of audio-only dialogue systems. In a 6-day in-the-wild deployment, 15.3\% of robot utterances were initiated by users' nonverbal behaviors rather than spoken input. Based on an analysis of observed customer behaviors, we define a set of frequent, service-relevant nonverbal cues and develop a real-time multi-person, multi-label recognizer that runs online from video. We then propose a dialogue framework that conditions LLM-based utterance generation on recognized nonverbal cue tokens, and optionally leverages a vision-language model when items are shown, enabling proactive robot responses without hand-crafted rules. We evaluate the approach offline on nonverbal-triggered turns and demonstrate an online prototype that reacts to users' nonverbal cues in real time.

</details>


### [270] [DA-Nav: Direction-Aware City-Scale Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11638)
*Ye Yuan,Kehan Chen,Xinqiang Yu,Wentao Xu,Heng Wang,Libo Huang,Chuanguang Yang,Yan Huang,Jiawei He,Zhulin An*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-Nav框架，利用商业导航工具（如Google Maps）的方向指令进行城市场景导航，将其转化为二维图像平面上的空间定位问题，并引入ReDA数据集支持链式思维推理轨迹恢复，在CARLA仿真和真实机器人上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级户外导航过度依赖稠密地图或昂贵的导航监督信号，缺乏对低成本、易获取的商业导航指令的有效利用。

Method: 提出DA-Nav框架，将导航重新定义为自我中心2D图像平面上的离散空间定位问题；设计包含偏差评估、动作预测和目标网格选择的Chain-of-Thought推理过程实现轨迹恢复；并构建ReDA数据集提供方向感知指令和恢复轨迹。

Result: 在CARLA仿真中，DA-Nav在未见过的城市环境中达到56.16%的成功率，超越现有最先进方法；无需微调即可直接适配四足机器人和人形机器人，实现公里级闭环户外导航。

Conclusion: DA-Nav通过方向感知视觉语言导航范式有效降低了城市导航对稠密地图的依赖，具备强大的轨迹恢复能力和跨机器人平台的可迁移性。

Abstract: City-scale outdoor navigation is currently hindered by the heavy reliance on dense maps or costly navigation supervision. In this work, we introduce a novel paradigm for leveraging directional instructions from commercial navigation tools (e.g., Google Maps). To bridge the gap between commercial instructions and executable navigation actions, while mitigating long-horizon error accumulation through robust trajectory recovery, we propose DA-Nav, a Direction-Aware vision-language Navigation framework that reformulates navigation as a discrete spatial grounding problem on the egocentric 2D image plane. To achieve trajectory recovery, DA-Nav employs a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning process encompassing deviation assessment, action prediction, and target grid selection. We further introduce ReDA, a dataset that provides direction-aware instructions and recovery trajectories to enhance spatial grounding and support CoT recovery reasoning. Extensive experiments in CARLA demonstrate that DA-Nav achieves a high success rate of 56.16% in unseen urban environments, outperforming existing State-of-The-Art (SoTA) methods while maintaining a substantially stronger recovery capability. Furthermore, without fine-tuning, DA-Nav seamlessly adapts to both quadruped and humanoid robots, enabling stable kilometer-scale closed-loop outdoor navigation in complex real world environments.

</details>


### [271] [Xiaomi-Robotics-U0: Unified Embodied Synthesis with World Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11643)
*Xinghang Li,Jun Guo,Qiwei Li,Long Qian,Hang Lai,Yueze Wang,Hongyu Yan,Jiahang Cao,Xi Chen,Jingen Qu,Jiaxi Song,Nan Sun,Hanye Zhao,Futeng Liu,Wanli Peng,Heyun Wang,Yunhong Wang,Caoyu Xia,Jack Zhao,Diyun Xiang,Hangjun Ye,Heng Qu,Huaping Liu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Xiaomi-Robotics-U0是一个380亿参数的多模态自回归模型，将基础图像/视频生成扩展到具身智能场景，支持多视角场景生成、具身迁移和具身视频生成，在多个任务上达到SOTA，并将机器人操作成功率从36.9%提升到63.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础图像/视频生成模型在具身场景中受限于多视角一致性、几何一致性和机器人具身约束，而传统方法用有限的机器人数据微调模型会损失大规模预训练获得的视觉知识，因此需要一种既能保留通用视觉知识又能适应具身场景的统一框架。

Method: 论文提出Xiaomi-Robotics-U0，一个380亿参数的多模态自回归模型，将具身生成视为基础图像/视频生成的扩展，联合优化文本到图像生成、图像编辑、具身场景生成、具身迁移和具身视频生成五个任务，采用统一框架保持预训练世界基础模型的泛化能力。

Result: 该模型在单步和序列生成任务上达到SOTA，在具身场景生成和迁移的人类评估中超越GPT-Image-2.0，在World Arena具身视频生成排行榜上排名第一，并将pi_0.5在真实世界操作任务上的分布外成功率从36.9%提升到63.2%。

Conclusion: 基础世界模型可以作为具身世界模型和可扩展的数据引擎用于具身智能，表明统一的多模态自回归建模是连接基础生成模型与具身智能的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent foundation image and video generation models offer strong generalization and controllability, but their direct application to embodied scenarios is limited by requirements for multi-view consistency, geometric coherence, and robot embodiment constraints. Existing methods typically adapt foundation models with limited robot data, often sacrificing visual knowledge acquired during large-scale pre-training. We present Xiaomi-Robotics-U0, a 38-billion-parameter multimodal autoregressive model for unified embodied synthesis. It treats embodied generation as an extension of foundation image and video generation and jointly optimizes text-to-image generation, image editing, embodied scene generation, embodied transfer, and embodied video generation. This unified framework preserves the generalization of the pre-trained world foundation model while adapting it to embodied settings. Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 is the first model to support high-quality multi-view scene generation across multiple robot embodiments and to introduce structured, controllable embodied transfer for fine-grained editing while preserving multi-view consistency and interaction dynamics. It achieves state-of-the-art results on single-step and sequential generation tasks, outperforming GPT-Image-2.0 in human evaluations of embodied scene generation and transfer, ranking first on World Arena for embodied video generation, and improving the out-of-distribution success rate of pi_0.5 from 36.9% to 63.2% on challenging real-world manipulation tasks. These results show that foundation world models can serve both as embodied world models and scalable data engines for embodied intelligence. Code and checkpoints are available at https://robotics.xiaomi.com/xiaomi-robotics-u0.html.

</details>


### [272] [Coordinated Incremental Trajectory Tracking of a Tailsitter Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11651)
*Evangelos Ntouros,Ewoud J. J. Smeur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋转矩阵的尾座式无人机微分平坦变换方法，解决了在协调飞行条件下含显著垂直速度分量的飞行控制问题，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微分平坦控制方法在尾座式无人机存在显著垂直速度分量的飞行场景下不再适用，而传统欧拉角表示存在歧义性，因此需要一种更鲁棒的解析变换框架。

Method: 利用简化气动模型，完全基于旋转矩阵推导了协调飞行条件下尾座式无人机的解析微分平坦变换，避免了欧拉角的模糊性。

Result: 通过包含显著垂直速度分量的飞行轨迹实验，验证了所提框架相较于原有方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了微分平坦控制器的适用范围，使其能够处理垂直速度较大的飞行状态，为尾座式无人机在更复杂机动条件下的控制提供了理论支持。

Abstract: This paper derives an analytical differential flatness transform for a tailsitter Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) under coordinated flight conditions using a simplified aerodynamic model. The proposed framework is formulated exclusively using rotation matrices, avoiding the ambiguities inherent to Euler angle representations. The method extends the applicability of an existing state-of-the-art differential flatness-based controller to flight regimes involving a significant vertical velocity component, where the previous approach becomes inapplicable. The proposed framework is validated experimentally with trajectories that highlight its advantages in these regimes.

</details>


### [273] [A Model for Mediating Multi-Modal Human Intent into Safe Maneuvers for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11654)
*Sofia Nelson,Dalal Alrajeh,Pedro Antonio Alarcon Granadeno,Jane Cleland-Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于需求约束的无人机机动响应模型，将多模态人类意图（语音、手势等）视为有界机动请求而非直接命令，通过请求-评估-执行管道，结合地形、间隔、飞行包线等约束进行安全验证与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接执行多模态操作输入（语音、手势等）在动态环境中存在安全风险，操作请求可能与地形约束、无人机间距或飞行包线限制冲突，需要一种能保障安全的意图中介机制。

Method: 构建需求约束的机动响应模型，将操作输入映射为受约束的运动基元，通过请求-评估-执行管道处理：先解析请求并计算置信度，再针对地形、间距、工作空间和飞行包线进行验证，最后选择约束执行、拒绝执行或在运行时监控下执行。利用前置条件、不变量、守卫条件和后置条件形式化机动基元的规范。

Result: 初步实验室验证表明，基于语音和GUI的输入能够被可靠地解释并作为受约束的机动请求安全执行。

Conclusion: 该模型通过需求形式化与运行时验证，有效弥合了多模态人机交互与无人机安全自主飞行之间的鸿沟，为未来的反应式综合方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Direct human interaction with autonomous UAV systems can be enabled through modalities such as speech, gestures, and graphical interfaces. However, interpreting such inputs as directly executable commands introduces safety risks in dynamic environments. Operator requests may conflict with terrain constraints, inter-UAV separation requirements, or flight-envelope limitations. In this paper, we present a requirements-governed maneuver-response model that mediates multi-modal human intent into safe UAV maneuvers by treating operator inputs as bounded maneuver requests rather than direct commands. Requested maneuvers are mapped to constrained motion primitives and processed through a structured request-evaluate-execute pipeline. Each request is interpreted with associated confidence, validated against terrain, separation, workspace, and flight-envelope constraints, and either constrained, rejected, or executed under continuous runtime monitoring. We further formalize the approach as a requirements-based specification model in which maneuver primitives are associated with explicit preconditions, invariants, guard conditions, and postconditions governing admissibility, execution safety, and emergency handling. These requirements support runtime verification and future reactive synthesis approaches. We present an initial lab-based validation demonstrating that voice and GUI-based inputs can be reliably interpreted and safely executed as constrained maneuver requests.

</details>


### [274] [Trajectory Planning and Certification for 3-DOF Robot Manipulators Using Real Quantifier Elimination Based on Comprehensive Gröbner Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11657)
*Yu Nakai,Akira Terui,Masahiko Mikawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CGS-QE方法的3自由度机械臂轨迹规划与认证算法，通过参数化系统的综合Gröbner基避免重复计算，提高效率，并在Risa/Asir中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹规划中求解逆运动学问题需要在每个轨迹点重新计算Gröbner基，耗时严重，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 采用基于Comprehensive Gröbner Systems的实量词消去（CGS-QE）方法，将末端执行器坐标作为参数计算CGS，避免逐点重复计算Gröbner基；利用CGS-QE方法认证轨迹上每点是否存在逆运动学解。

Result: 在直线段和三次自然样条轨迹上均能高效完成轨迹规划并认证解的存在性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过参数化建模和CGS有效提升了轨迹规划与认证效率，可推广至更复杂的轨迹类型。

Abstract: We propose an algorithm and its implementation for trajectory planning and certification for 3-DOF robot manipulators. The method uses Real Quantifier Elimination (QE) based on Comprehensive Gröbner Systems (CGS), also known as the CGS-QE method. The main advantage of the proposed method is its efficiency in trajectory planning and solution certification. This efficiency comes from the effective use of the CGS. First, for trajectory planning, we solve the inverse kinematics problem at each point along the trajectory via Gröbner basis computation. This usually requires recalculating the Gröbner basis at every point, which is time-consuming. We avoid this by computing the CGS for a parametric system. Here, the end-effector coordinates are parameters. This approach streamlines the algorithm. Second, for solution certification, the CGS-QE method certifies that an inverse kinematics solution exists at any point along the end-effector's trajectory. Our method also certifies solutions for trajectories composed of line segments and cubic natural splines. The algorithm is implemented within the computer algebra system Risa/Asir.

</details>


### [275] [Self-Healing Visual Recovery for Autonomous Ground Vehicles Using Camera-Only Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11686)
*Jakob Solberg Berntzen,Safia Fatima,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的两阶段恢复方法，使仅配备摄像头的低成本无人地面车辆（UGV）能够在丢失引导线后自主恢复跟踪，无需LiDAR、GPS或GPU支持。系统在CPU上以20Hz运行，在119次故障注入实验中成功率达86.6%，中位恢复时间3.26秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本UGV在仓库、巡检走廊等室内环境中依赖地面引导线导航，但现有方案在引导线被遮挡或急转弯时容易失效。传感器丰富的平台可通过硬件冗余应对，但仅依赖摄像头的系统缺乏有效的运行时恢复机制。

Method: 提出两阶段恢复方法：第一阶段，丢失引导线后原地旋转并逐步放宽颜色检测阈值，多帧确认后尝试重新捕获；第二阶段，若仍未找到线，利用单目视觉里程计沿保存的面包屑位置回退后重试。系统集成深度门控HSV线跟踪器、YOLOv8n障碍物检测器和视觉里程记面包屑映射器，在单一控制周期（50ms）内嵌入完整MAPE-K自适应循环。

Result: 在Webots仿真环境中的三个不同课程上进行了119次故障注入实验，成功率为86.6%，中位恢复时间为3.26秒。

Conclusion: 实验证明，在仅配备摄像头的低成本UGV上实现可靠的视觉恢复是可行的，满足实际应用中的成本和计算限制。

Abstract: Low-cost unmanned ground vehicles are often used in indoor places like warehouses, inspection corridors, and farm rows, where painted floor lines guide the robot. Line following is useful because it only needs one camera and little computing power, but it can fail when the line is blocked or turns sharply and goes out of view. Sensor-rich platforms tolerate this through hardware redundancy (LiDAR, GPS, multiple cameras), but camera-only systems must recover at runtime with no additional infrastructure. This paper presents a lightweight, two-stage recovery approach that restores guideline tracking without LiDAR, GPS, or a GPU. When the line is lost, the robot first turns in place while slowly relaxing its color checks and waiting for confirmation across multiple frames (Stage 1). If the line is still not found, monocular visual odometry moves the robot back to saved breadcrumb positions before it tries again (Stage 2). The system uses a depth-gated HSV line tracker, a YOLOv8n obstacle detector, and a visual odometry breadcrumb mapper, and it runs at 20 Hz on CPU-only hardware. The controller embeds a complete MAPE-K loop within a single 50 ms control tick, with no external adaptation manager required. The approach is evaluated across 119 fault-injected episodes on three Webots simulation courses. The method was successful in 86.6% of cases, with a median recovery time of 3.26 seconds. These results demonstrate that reliable visual recovery is feasible on camera-only UGVs within practical cost and computational limits.

</details>


### [276] [Automated Synthesis of Facial Mechanisms for Conversational Animatronic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11688)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Zi Lin,Jiawen Yang,Ziqiao Peng,Junyan Lao,Lin Cheng,Huazhe Xu,Hang Zhao,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化、可自动化设计的联动式机械人脸模板，能够从单张2D肖像输入快速生成可制造的内部机械结构，并配套开发了双身份对话式面部运动合成框架，实现大规模个性化机械人脸的高效合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟人机械人脸是定制化系统，每种新面部几何都需要大量手动机械重新设计，导致大规模个性化成本高、速度慢。

Method: 提出参数化联动式机械人脸模板（支持拓扑和布局的系统化缩放与重定向）；层次化自动设计算法（从2D肖像→3D重建→无碰撞可制造机制）；解剖引导运动体积+AU轨迹表达性目标+碰撞驱动优化；双身份对话式面部运动合成框架（联合建模说话与倾听行为）。

Result: 通过自动机构合成定量评估、与手动设计对比、对话式运动合成基准测试和实时部署、用户感知研究等多维度实验验证系统有效性。

Conclusion: 实现了从单张2D肖像到可物理执行机械人脸的全自动合成，并支持双向多轮对话场景，为大规模个性化社交机器人面部设计提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Animatronic faces are a central component of socially interactive robots, enabling rich nonverbal communication through facial articulation. However, state-of-the-art animatronic faces are typically tailored systems: each new facial geometry requires extensive manual mechanical redesign, making large-scale personalization prohibitively slow and costly. In this work, we pursue automated and scalable mechanical face synthesis, aiming to rapidly generate a physically realizable facial mechanism for a wide range of facial geometries. We introduce a parametric, linkage-driven mechanical face template whose topology and actuator layout are explicitly parameterized to support systematic scaling and retargeting across diverse facial morphologies. Building on this template, we propose a hierarchical automatic design algorithm that takes a single 2D portrait as input, reconstructs a target 3D face, and synthesizes a collision-free, manufacturable internal mechanism. The algorithm combines anatomy-guided feasible motion volumes, Action Unit (AU)-derived trajectory-based expressiveness objectives, and a collision-driven outer-loop refinement strategy. Beyond hardware synthesis, we argue that future mechanical faces deployed at scale must engage in bidirectional, multi-turn conversation rather than functioning solely as speaking or listening heads. To this end, we develop a dual-identity conversational facial motion synthesis framework that jointly models speaking and listening behaviors from audio, producing temporally coherent 3D facial motion suitable for physical execution. We validate our system through extensive experiments, including (i) quantitative evaluation of automatic mechanism synthesis across diverse facial geometries, (ii) comparisons against manual mechanical design, (iii) benchmarks on conversational facial motion synthesis and real-time deployment, and (iv) perceptual user studies.

</details>


### [277] [From World Action Models to Embodied Brains: A Roadmap for Open-World Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11689)
*Yuanzhi Liang,Xufeng Zhan,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文回顾了世界动作模型（WAMs）在通用人工智能物理智能方向的发展，指出其面临角色表示、目标标准化和系统组合三大鸿沟，并提出以"具身大脑"为核心、结合物理约束和共享协议的模块化物理智能堆栈路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作模型、视觉-语言-动作策略和世界模型在推动物理智能方面虽有进展，但存在行动空间不兼容、预测目标不一致、数据集/任务规范各异、运行时系统接口有限等问题，导致发展碎片化，亟需系统化梳理和统一框架。

Method: 通过综述WAMs的演化历程，归纳出三个耦合的鸿沟（模型角色与表示、目标与标准化、系统组合），进而提出共同演化路线图：以"具身大脑"为长期模型目标，整合多模态上下文、比较候选干预、发出状态转换或能力请求而非直接指令；通过物理约束（工具、控制器、验证、日志追踪）接地模型输出；利用共享协议对齐异构模型/数据/任务/具身形态；闭环后训练将验证后的交互转化为可复用经验。

Result: 提出了一个模块化的物理智能堆栈，包含具身大脑、物理约束、共享协议和闭环后训练四个核心组件，旨在构建自适应和自我改进的具身智能体。

Conclusion: WAMs为预测功能提供了有前景的原型，但需要协同演进框架来解决碎片化问题；该路线图为物理智能的标准化和模块化发展提供了系统方向。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence ultimately requires agents that can reason and act in the physical world. Action models, vision-language-action policies, and world models have advanced this goal, while World Action Models (WAMs) are particularly promising because they connect candidate interventions with predicted consequences. However, progress remains fragmented: models use incompatible action spaces and prediction targets, datasets and tasks follow different conventions, and runtime systems expose limited interfaces for reuse and evaluation. We review the evolution toward WAMs and organize these limitations into three coupled gaps: model roles and representations, objectives and standardization, and system composition. Building on this analysis, we propose a co-evolution roadmap for physical intelligence centered on the \emph{embodied brain}, a long-term model target for integrating multimodal context, comparing candidate interventions, and issuing state-transition or capability requests rather than direct actuator commands. WAMs provide promising prototypes for its predictive functions, while a physical harness grounds model outputs through tools, controllers, verification, and trace logging. Shared contracts align heterogeneous models, data, tasks, and embodiments, and closed-loop post-training converts verified interaction into reusable experience. Together, these components define a modular physical-intelligence stack for adaptive and self-improving embodied agents.

</details>


### [278] [Requirement-Driven Design of Whole-Body Social Tactile Sensing via Virtual Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11690)
*Dakarai Crowder,Ruohan Zhang,Alexis E. Block,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从交互数据中推导触觉传感需求（空间分辨率和布局）的框架，通过VR平台收集高分辨率全身接触分布数据，识别出9种社交触摸手势，并在18名参与者中采集了5,520组数据，为类人机器人平台提供了皮肤覆盖和传感器密度的定量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感设计采用硬件驱动方式，预定义的传感器配置限制了覆盖范围、空间分辨率和可识别手势类型，缺乏从实际交互需求出发的设计方法。

Method: 1) 基于VR和触觉反馈平台收集高分辨率全身接触分布数据；2) 识别出9种重复出现的社交触摸手势；3) 选择8种手势进行18名受试者的受控数据采集（5,520组）；4) 分析接触分布和模拟触觉编码，建立皮肤覆盖和传感器密度的定量基线。

Result: 成功识别9种社交触摸手势，建立了5,520组开源数据集，提供了类人机器人平台触觉传感的定量基线。

Conclusion: 该方法可迁移至其他机器人形态，在硬件制造前推导出形态特定的传感需求。

Abstract: Tactile sensing for social-physical human-robot interaction (spHRI) is designed in a hardware-driven manner, where predefined sensor configurations constrain coverage, spatial resolution, and the range of recognizable gestures. We propose a requirement-driven framework that derives sensing requirements, specifically spatial resolution and placement, directly from interaction data. Using a VR-based platform with haptic feedback, we collected high-resolution whole-body contact distributions across multiple social scenarios, from which we identified nine recurring social touch gestures. Eight gestures were selected for controlled data collection with 18 participants, yielding an open-source dataset of 5,520 trials. Analysis of contact distributions and simulated tactile encodings provides quantitative baselines for skin coverage and sensor density on a humanoid robot platform. While demonstrated on a single robot platform, the methodology is designed to be transferable to other robot morphologies, potentially enabling morphology-specific sensing requirements to be derived prior to hardware fabrication.

</details>


### [279] [High-level spatial Dubins airplane-based reference smoothing with low-level geometric tracking for quadrotor control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11724)
*Mogens Plessen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Dubins飞机模型（高层路径平滑）和几何跟踪控制器（底层跟踪）的四旋翼控制方法，用于在横向约束下（如沿障碍物轮廓）跟踪参考路径，支持离线单次规划或在线滚动规划两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四旋翼在横向约束（如沿障碍物轮廓）下跟踪参考路径的问题，同时降低计算复杂度。

Method: 高层：基于Dubins飞机模型进行降维和解耦，通过线性规划实现路径平滑；底层：基于完整四旋翼模型的几何跟踪控制器进行参考跟踪。

Result: 所提方法能有效处理横向约束下的路径跟踪，支持离线与在线两种规划模式。

Conclusion: 通过双层架构（Dubins模型+几何控制）实现了在横向约束下的高效四旋翼路径跟踪控制。

Abstract: A method for the control of quadrotors is presented. It is composed of a high-level reference smoothing step and a low-level reference tracking step. The high-level step leverages the Dubins airplane model for dimensionality reduction and reduced computational complexity, and exploits its structure for decoupling, spatial modeling and the formulation of a small linear program. The low-level step leverages a geometric tracking controller, which is based on the full quadrotor model. The method is designed for the tracking of references subject to lateral constraints along the path. An example is the tracking of references along obstacle contours. It is differentiated between two different setups. Either the high-level planning step is conducted once and offline, or, alternatively, the high-level planning step is conducted recedingly online in closed-loop over a limited spatial prediction horizon.

</details>


### [280] [NeuralActuator: Neural Actuation Modeling for Robot Dynamics and External Force Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11734)
*Zhiyang Dou,John U. Onyemelukwe,Hangxing Zhang,Heng Zhang,Minghao Guo,Yunsheng Tian,Michal Piotr Lipiec,Joshua Jacob,Chao Liu,Peter Yichen Chen,Yuri Ivanov,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuralActuator，一种神经执行器模型，可联合预测力矩替代量、外力与电机状态，以解决低成本机器人平台因执行器非线性动力学导致的仿真与现实差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同iable仿真器推动了策略学习与模型预测控制的发展，但执行器动力学（如摩擦、迟滞、回差、热效应）导致的仿真与现实差异问题在低成本平台上尤为严重，传统线性电流-力矩关系τ=KtI变得不可靠。

Method: 提出NeuralActuator模型：1) 预测广义力矩替代量用于轨迹传播；2) 结合接触概率门预测外力；3) 输出电机状态评分。使用变分仿真训练力矩头（无需直接标签），其他头接受直接监督。Transformer捕捉时序依赖并支持实时推理。同时发布Neural Actuation Dataset (NAD)。

Result: 在三个不同成本平台（OpenManipulator-X约$500、SO-101约$3000、Franka Panda约$30000）上评估，覆盖三种执行器系列。低成本平台验证了动力学与力估计性能，Franka提供了额外负载力估计基准。还展示了电机状态估计应用及NeuralActuator作为预训练模块提升行为克隆性能的效果。

Conclusion: NeuralActuator可有效弥合执行器动力学层面的仿真-现实差异，适用于不同成本的机器人平台，并可作为预训练模块提升下游策略学习性能。

Abstract: Differentiable simulators have advanced policy learning and model-based control, yet actuator dynamics remain an important source of sim-to-real error. This is particularly acute on low-cost platforms, where the linear current-to-torque relation $τ= K_tI$ becomes unreliable during commanded-target tracking because of friction, hysteresis, backlash, and thermal effects. We present NeuralActuator, a neural actuator model that jointly predicts (i) a simulator-equivalent generalized-effort surrogate for trajectory propagation on low-cost servo platforms, (ii) external force with a contact-probability gate for sensorless force perception, and (iii) a motor-condition score for the supervised joint. We also introduce the Neural Actuation Dataset (NAD), collected with a twin-arm teleoperation system that records robot states and actuator telemetry together with external-force labels. The torque-surrogate head is trained through differentiable simulation from pose trajectories without direct generalized-effort labels, while the force, gate, and motor-condition heads receive direct supervision. A Transformer captures temporal dependencies while supporting real-time inference. We evaluate NeuralActuator on a 5-DoF OpenManipulator-X, a 6-DoF SO-101, and a 7-DoF Franka Emika Panda, spanning three actuator families and platforms costing approximately USD 500 to over USD 30,000. The low-cost platforms support dynamics and force evaluation, while the offline Franka experiment provides an additional payload-force-estimation benchmark. Experiments further demonstrate its application for motor condition estimation on OpenManipulator-X and improved behavior-cloning performance when NeuralActuator is used as a pretrained module.

</details>


### [281] [AutoPath: Learning Transferable Goal-Conditioned Stochastic Path Prior for Safe Navigation Without Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11739)
*Ziyang Zhang,Boyang Zhou,Zesong Yang,Haocheng Peng,Zeming Gai,Xiao Liang,Yujun Shen,Danping Zou,Ruizhen Hu,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可迁移目标条件随机路径先验的导航方法，通过学习旋转不变的路径分布，实现多模态可行路径的采样，并在不同机器人平台上无需重新训练即可迁移应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱动态环境中实现实时导航需要处理多模态可行路径，且现有方法受限于机器人特定运动约束，缺乏跨平台的可迁移性。

Method: 1) 将导航建模为学习可迁移的目标条件随机路径先验；2) 提出目标对齐的规范状态表示，消除平面旋转模糊性；3) 利用几何感知的极坐标动作流形参数化局部路径；4) 引入风险敏感的效用塑形与多目标分布 rollout。

Result: 在密集静态环境和动态行人场景中持续实现高成功率与竞争性效率，且将从差分驱动机器人上学到的路径先验直接迁移到四足平台无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化路径先验学习框架能有效处理多模态导航行为，同时具备良好的跨平台泛化能力和安全感知能力。

Abstract: Real-time navigation in cluttered and dynamic environments requires collision-free and dynamically feasible motion under limited perception. However, feasible navigation behaviors are inherently multimodal because multiple paths may exist around obstacles. In this paper, we formulate navigation as learning a transferable goal-conditioned stochastic path prior that models a reusable distribution over goal-aligned geometry-consistent local paths conditioned on local observations. This formulation enables structured sampling of navigation candidates, allowing multiple feasible paths to be explored through sampling without relying on robot-specific motion constraints. To this end, we introduce a goal-aligned canonical state representation that removes in-plane rotational ambiguity and normalizes local geometry with respect to the goal, enabling rotation-invariant path distribution learning. We further develop a structured prior learning framework that parameterizes local paths using a geometry-aware polar action manifold and incorporates risk-sensitive utility shaping with multi-goal distributional rollouts for stable and safety-aware planning. Extensive experiments in dense static environments and dynamic pedestrian scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method achieves consistently high success rates with competitive efficiency while enabling cross-platform transfer of a single path prior learned on differential-drive robots to quadruped platforms without retraining.

</details>


### [282] [A Compact Top-Loading Robot for Endovascular Interventions: Design, Control and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11779)
*Jonas Fischer,Lennart Karstensen,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑型机器人系统，用于血管内介入手术。该系统采用两个交替移动的推车，配合气动膜片夹爪和旋转齿轮，能够对标准血管内器械（如导丝、导管）进行连续平移和旋转操作。顶部装载设计方便器械快速更换。在运动跟踪实验中，平移和旋转的平均相对跟踪误差分别为3.6%和4.1%；在体外血管模型中，机器人辅助导航成功到达目标，验证了系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人辅助血管内介入系统存在患者侧设置受限、灵活性不足、器械交换复杂等问题，导致临床流程整合困难。为此，本文旨在开发一种紧凑型机器人系统，实现对标准血管内器械的连续操控，并简化器械交换流程，以更好地融入临床工作流。

Method: 系统由两个交替移动的推车组成，每个推车集成气动膜片夹爪和旋转齿轮。通过“领导者-跟随者”控制策略，克服单次行程有限的问题，实现器械的连续运动。顶部装载设计使得导丝、导管等器械无需更改机器人设置即可快速更换。

Result: 运动跟踪实验显示，导丝和导管的平移和旋转运动轨迹平滑，平移平均相对跟踪误差为3.6%，旋转为4.1%。在体外血管模型实验中，机器人辅助导航在大多数试验中成功到达目标。

Conclusion: 本系统在台架和体外实验中证明了对标准血管内器械进行连续操控的技术可行性。其紧凑的顶部装载设计有望简化器械交换并促进临床工作流整合。未来工作将致力于改进夹持性能、驱动速度、力反馈，并在更接近临床的环境中进行评估。

Abstract: Robot-assisted endovascular intervention can potentially reduce radiation exposure, improve surgeon ergonomics, enable telesurgery, support active assistance and autonomy, and enhance procedural precision. However, existing systems often suffer from limited procedural coverage because constrained patient-side setups, restricted flexibility, and complex instrument exchange hinder clinical workflow integration. This work presents a compact robotic system for endovascular interventions that enables continuous translational and rotational manipulation of standard endovascular instruments. The system consists of two alternating carts with pneumatically actuated membrane grippers integrated into rotating gripper gears. Its top-loading design allows rapid exchange of instruments such as guidewires and catheters without changing the robotic setup. A leader-follower control strategy enables continuous motion despite the finite stroke of each cart. The system was evaluated in motion-tracking experiments with guidewires and catheters and in an in vitro vascular phantom. The motion-tracking experiments showed generally smooth translational and rotational motion profiles. Across all tested guidewire and catheter experiments, the mean relative tracking errors were 3.6% for translational motion and 4.1% for rotational motion. In the vascular phantom, robot-assisted navigation reached the target in most trials, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed manipulation concept under in vitro conditions. The presented robotic system demonstrates technical feasibility for continuous manipulation of standard endovascular instruments in bench-top and in vitro experiments. The compact top-loading design may ease instrument exchange and clinical workflow integration. Future work will focus on improving gripping performance, actuation speed, force feedback, and evaluation in more clinically realistic settings.

</details>


### [283] [MIRA: A Modular Open-Source Micro-UAV for Indoor Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11785)
*Lucas K. de Oliveira,Felipe A. G. Tommaselli,João Aires Marsicano,Marco S. Tayar,Pedro A. R. Saraiva,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRA是一个低成本、开源、模块化的室内微无人机平台，基于3D打印PLA机身和容器化软件架构，实现了副处理器到自驾仪的低延迟通信（中位延迟0.02ms），结构振动集中在90-110Hz频段，不影响控制带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内微无人机研究面临两难：商用平台不开放底层接口无法深度修改，而从头定制需要大量工程投入才能开始飞行测试，限制了实验室的研究范围。

Method: 提出MIRA架构：采用3D打印PLA机身实现低成本复现；通过容器化软件和Micro XRCE-DDS协议管理副处理器与自驾仪之间的通信桥；采用白盒架构设计，核心子系统可独立替换而无需重构固件。

Result: 在光学动捕环境下手动位置控制飞行测试中，通信管道中位延迟仅0.02ms；功率谱密度分析显示结构振动能量集中在90-110Hz窄带内，与低于20Hz的控制带宽隔离，且在自驾仪安全阈值内。

Conclusion: MIRA通过模块化开源设计，降低了室内无人机研究的入门门槛，支持本地制造和组件替换，为实验室提供了一个灵活、低成本的研究平台。

Abstract: Indoor robotics research increasingly relies on micro-UAVs whose airframe, electronics, and control software are fully open to modification. Off-the-shelf platforms rarely expose the low-level access required for such modifications, while building a custom alternative typically requires substantial engineering effort before flight testing can begin, leaving many laboratories to work within constraints that limit the scope of their research. We present MIRA (Modular Indoor Research Architecture), a low-cost, open-source micro-UAV for indoor research built around a replicable 3D-printed PLA airframe and a containerized low-level software package managing the companion-to-autopilot communication bridge via Micro XRCE-DDS. Designed as a white-box architecture, core subsystems are individually replaceable without firmware refactoring, supporting local fabrication and component substitution from existing lab inventory. We characterize MIRA through manual flight in position-control mode within an optical motion-capture volume, where the communication pipeline sustains a median companion-to-autopilot latency of 0.02 ms and power spectral density analysis confirms the structural vibration energy stays concentrated in a narrow 90 to 110 Hz band, isolated from the sub-20 Hz control bandwidth and within the autopilot safety thresholds.

</details>


### [284] [Casting Everything to Online API Services? A Survey of Integrating Localized Speech Recognition Models in Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11792)
*Sheng Li,Jing Li,Felix Schijve,Jun Hu,Emilia Barakova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了自动语音识别（ASR）技术在机器人系统中的应用，涵盖从传统方法到Whisper等深度学习模型的演进、大规模数据集与开源工具、部署策略（ROS、云端、混合方案）以及实际机器人平台，最后讨论了部署挑战与多模态交互的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统中ASR应用广泛，但现有综述缺乏对ASR技术如何系统性地集成到机器人平台（特别是ROS、云端、混合方案）的全面梳理，本文旨在填补这一空白，帮助社交机器人研究者更好地导航语言驱动的自然人机交互领域。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，从三个维度展开：(1) ASR模型家族（从传统方法到Whisper等深度学习模型）；(2) 机器人部署策略（基于ROS、云端、混合方案）；(3) 真实机器人平台案例。同时整理了大规模数据集和开源工具。

Result: 系统梳理了ASR在机器人领域的集成现状，总结了不同部署策略的优缺点，列举了主流开源工具和数据集，并指出现有系统在嘈杂动态环境下的鲁棒性不足等挑战。

Conclusion: ASR已成为机器人自然交互的关键技术，未来方向包括：多模态融合、提升在多样化动态环境中的鲁棒性，以及更高效的端侧部署方案。本文为社交机器人研究者提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has become a critical component of modern robotic systems because it is one of the most natural and intuitive ways for humans to interact with robots. A commonly used method is to directly use API services online. But is that all we can do? This article provides an overview of how ASR technologies are integrated into various intelligent robots and machines. We discuss the evolution of speech recognition from established approaches to state-of-the-art deep learning models, such as OpenAI's Whisper. We also list large-scale datasets and open source toolkits that have been widely used in both industry and academia. We structure the survey around ASR model families, deployment strategies in robotics (especially ROS-based, cloud-based, and hybrid solutions), and several real-world robotic platforms. Finally, we outline the challenges of deploying robust speech recognition in robots and discuss future directions, including multimodal interaction in diverse and dynamic environments. This paper can help social robotics researchers better navigate the emerging domain of language-based natural human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [285] [Robust bipedal locomotion on flowable slopes via foot-driven terrain manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11855)
*Deniz Kerimoglu,Junnosuke Kamohara,Jiyeon Maeng,Ziwon Yoon,Seth Hutchinson,Ye Zhao,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过控制足部与颗粒状坡面（如沙坡）的相互作用来提升双足机器人行走稳定性。研究发现，足底设置的尖刺（cleats）间距适中时，可优化力分布、避免超过土壤屈服阈值，从而实现最高30度坡面的稳定行走，并据此设计了可主动调节刺深度的足部结构，在小型和大型机器人上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在刚性地面上已有成熟的控制策略，但在流态化地面（如颗粒状坡面）上，足部接触会引发大变形和固-液转变，导致传统控制方法失效。缺乏有效描述流态化地形动力学的方法，使得在设计运动时难以考虑地形影响。

Method: 通过小型（1.4 kg）机器人物理实验，系统研究不同间距的足底尖刺（cleats）对颗粒坡面行走的影响，分析力分布与土壤屈服阈值的关系，找出最优间距。基于此原理设计可主动调节刺深度的足部，并在大型（15 kg）自主双足机器人上验证。

Result: 稀疏和密集的尖刺间距分别导致过度地形屈服和阻力过大，均造成性能下降或失败。中等间距可维持基底应力接近或低于屈服阈值，实现在高达30度的颗粒坡面上稳定行走。该原理在小型和大型机器人上均有效。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种以肢体为中心（limb-centric）的地形交互控制方法，替代传统以身体运动调节地形效应的思路，为双足机器人在复杂流态化地形上的运动设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Bipedal robots are challenging to control because they operate close to instability, where small variations in foot-terrain contact can rapidly destabilize locomotion. On rigid terrain, bipedal robots mitigate this fragility by using well-established contact mechanics and control strategies. On flowable surfaces such as granular slopes, foot contact can induce large surface deformations and solid-fluid-like transitions, coupling terrain effects with robot dynamics, leading to underperformance or failure. This is partly due to the lack of reliable methods to represent the dynamics of flowable terrain, making it difficult to account for terrain effects in locomotion design. Here, we investigate how controlling terrain response can improve bipedal locomotion on granular slopes by studying the terradynamics of cleated feet, thin plates emanating from the foot soles. Systematic studies of a small-scale (1.4 kg) robophysical biped reveal that cleats with sparse and dense spacing lead to excessive terrain yielding and resistance, respectively, degrading performance and leading to failure. An intermediate cleat spacing distributes interaction forces to maintain substrate stresses near (or below) the yield threshold, enabling walking on granular slopes up to 30 degrees. Guided by these principles, we design a foot that actively adjusts cleat depth and accommodates both rigid and granular terrain. We also demonstrate that the principles of effective foot-terrain interaction translate to a larger (15 kg) autonomous biped. Our study presents an alternative to conventional body-centric robot control approaches, which regulate terrain-induced effects through body motion, by instead regulating terrain interactions through limb-centric approach.

</details>


### [286] [A Minimalist Retargeting-Guided Reinforcement Learning Recipe for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11874)
*Yunhai Feng,Natalie Leung,Jiaxuan Wang,Lujie Yang,Haozhi Qi,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REGRIND，一种从单次人类演示中通过重定向引导的强化学习流水线，用于学习灵巧操作策略，成功在剪刀和螺丝刀等接触丰富任务上实现零样本迁移到真实多指机器人手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形全身控制方法（将人类运动重定向到机器人运动参考，再通过强化学习跟踪）在灵巧操作任务上的适用性尚不明确，因为操作涉及复杂的接触动力学和精细的接触模式力调节。

Method: 1) 将人手-物体运动重定向到保持空间和接触关系的机器人参考；2) 在仿真中训练残差强化学习策略以跟踪物体中心关键点；3) 通过系统辨识实现零样本迁移到真实硬件。

Result: 在两种多指手上成功实现剪刀操作和螺丝刀转动等接触丰富的工具使用任务，产生流畅类人行为，并通过系统硬件实验分析了仿真到真实迁移的关键因素。

Conclusion: 重定向引导的强化学习范式可有效迁移到灵巧操作任务，为接触丰富场景下的策略学习提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Recent work in humanoid whole-body control has found success with a simple recipe: retarget human motion to robot kinematic references, then train policies via reinforcement learning (RL) to track them. But how does this recipe transfer to dexterous manipulation? The answer is not obvious, as manipulation involves complex, contact-rich dynamics and requires delicate regulation of contact modes and forces. We present REGRIND, a minimalist retargeting-guided RL pipeline that learns dexterous manipulation policies from a single human demonstration. REGRIND retargets human hand-object motion to a robot reference that preserves hand-object spatial and contact relationships, trains a residual RL policy in simulation to track object-centric keypoints along that reference, and transfers the resulting policy zero-shot to hardware with careful system identification. The resulting policies produce fluid, human-like behavior on two different multi-fingered hands across contact-rich tool-use tasks, including operating a pair of scissors and turning a screwdriver. Through systematic hardware experiments, we identify and analyze the key factors that govern sim-to-real transfer in dexterous manipulation, offering practical guidance for retargeting-based learning in contact-rich settings. Videos and code are available at https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND.

</details>


### [287] [Mixture of Frames Policy: Multi-Frame Action Denoising for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11884)
*Dian Wang,Jisang Park,Xiaomeng Xu,Han Zhang,Shuran Song,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出混合帧策略（MoF），一种在多个坐标系下同步去噪的扩散策略，通过可微的SE(3)变换和帧专用去噪器，在九项仿真和两项真实双臂操作任务中优于单帧及MoE基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作本质上涉及多个坐标系（如末端执行器坐标系、基坐标系），但现有的扩散策略通常固定在单一动作帧上，导致去噪器需要建模在该帧下不必要的复杂动作分布。

Method: 维护单一规范扩散状态，将其重表达至多个任务相关帧，分别经帧专用去噪器处理，再将噪声预测融合回规范帧；引入基于列的6D旋转表示，支持精确可微的SE(3)帧变换。

Result: 在九项仿真双臂任务中，最优动作帧依赖于具体任务，MoF优于最优单帧选择与标准MoE基线；在两项真实世界双臂移动操控任务中，MoF超越所有单帧基线。

Conclusion: MoF通过多帧协同去噪机制自适应地选择最优动作表示，有效提升了双臂移动操作任务的性能，验证了动作帧选择对任务依赖性及多帧融合的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is inherently multi-frame: local actions may be simple in an end-effector frame, while transport, upright-object handling, and whole-body coordination are better represented in a base-aligned frame. However, modern diffusion-based visuomotor policies typically commit to a single predefined action frame, forcing one denoiser to model action distributions that are often unnecessarily complex in that frame. We propose Mixture of Frames Policy (MoF), a diffusion policy that performs synchronized action denoising across multiple coordinate frames. MoF maintains a single canonical diffusion state, re-expresses it in several task-relevant frames, applies frame-specialized denoisers, and fuses their noise predictions back in the canonical frame. To make this possible for intermediate noisy diffusion states, we introduce a column-based 6D rotation representation within an SE(3) action parameterization that supports exact, differentiable frame transformations without requiring noisy rotations to lie on the SO(3) manifold. Across nine simulated bimanual manipulation tasks, we show that the best action frame is task-dependent and that MoF improves over oracle frame selection and standard Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) baselines. We further evaluate MoF on two real-world bimanual mobile manipulation tasks, demonstrating that it outperforms all constituent single-frame baselines. Project homepage: https://mofpo.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [From ML Predictions to Informed Diagnostic Assistance Using the Toulmin Model of Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09664)
*Anca Marginean,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于图尔敏论证模型的图像诊断解释框架，将ML生成的视网膜诊断分解为主张、依据、保证、限定词、反驳和支撑六个组件，通过生物标志物提取、MedGemma医学知识分析、量化评估和MedSigLip图像相似度计算，为人类专家提供可解释的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ML诊断结果缺乏结构化、可解释的评估方式，仅依赖XAI方法或简单的论证方法不足以支持人类专家进行批判性判断。

Method: 采用图尔敏论证模型分解诊断过程：生物标志物提取模型提供"依据"；MedGemma代理分析"保证"（连接依据与主张的推理）；量化评估确定"限定词"；MedSigLip计算图像相似度构建"反驳"。

Result: 该框架将诊断的各论证组件呈现给人类专家，使专家能够对ML生成的诊断进行更知情和批判性的评估。

Conclusion: 基于论证模型的分解方法比单纯依赖XAI更能为人类专家提供结构化、可解释的诊断评估，有助于增强ML在医疗诊断中的可信度。

Abstract: To provide a structured and interpretable assessment, we decompose the image-based diagnosis into components following the Toulmin model of argumentation. This model consists of a claim, grounds, warrant, qualifier, rebuttal, and backing. Consider a claim generated by a machine learning (ML) model for retinal diagnosis. Rather than accepting this claim at face value, one could either apply explainable AI (XAI) methods or adopt an argumentation-based approach. In our framework, a model specialized in biomarker extraction from images provides the grounds. The warrant-linking the grounds to the claim - is analyzed by an agent equipped with medical knowledge; in our architecture, this role is fulfilled by a MedGemma agent. The qualifier is determined based on the overall quantitative evaluation of both the warrant and grounds models. Finally, a rebuttal is constructed using image similarity measures computed with MedSigLip. All these components are presented to the human expert, enabling a more informed and critical assessment of the ML-generated diagnosis.

</details>


### [289] [Format Sensitivity Index: Token-Controlled Prompt Wrapper Robustness and Schema Compliance in LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09665)
*Deep Pankajbhai Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提示包装器（Prompt wrappers）仅格式不同就能显著影响模型得分，甚至改变排行榜结论。本文提出两个新指标FSI和PSI来量化这种敏感性，发现模型间FSI平均差异超30倍，主要由合规失败解释。即使控制任务、模型和包装器，可解析性仍强力预测准确率。建议报告准确率时必须同时报告包装器方差和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示提示包装器（Prompt wrappers）因格式差异导致模型得分显著波动这一被忽视的问题，证明这种方差足以翻转排行榜结论，呼吁建立更稳健的评估标准。

Method: 在token控制协议下，提出格式敏感性指数（FSI）和可解析性敏感性指数（PSI）两个互补指标。在OpenRouter上收集14万次生成，覆盖7个QA任务、5种包装器家族、4个参数规模从7B到72B的指令模型，使用固定效应回归进行分析。

Result: 模型间平均FSI差异超过30倍，主要由合规失败（compliance failures）解释。可解析性即使控制任务、模型和包装器后，仍然是准确率的强预测因子。

Conclusion: 仅报告准确率而不报告包装器方差和合规性在统计上是脆弱的；针对基准测试和结构化输出部署给出实用建议。

Abstract: Prompt wrappers often differ only in formatting, yet they can change model scores enough to flip leaderboard conclusions. We study this variance under a token-controlled protocol and introduce two complementary metrics: the Format Sensitivity Index (FSI), the accuracy range induced by wrapper choice, and the Parseability Sensitivity Index (PSI), the corresponding range in answer parseability. Across 140,000 OpenRouter generations spanning 7 QA tasks, 5 wrapper families, and 4 instruct models from 7B to 72B parameters, we find that mean FSI varies by over 30x across models and is largely explained by compliance failures.
